Method: 提出了一个嵌套语言生成模型：形式化语言F（可通过成员查询oracle访问）中包含未知的有价值语言H ⊆ F，H的核心C（已有文献）以对抗性方式枚举，密度为α。将输出分为有价值（∈H）、琐碎（∈F\H）和幻觉（∉F）三类，并在该框架下对generation with breadth、sound coverage、最优覆盖率等概念进行了理论分析。

Result: 1) 验证器不能替代品味：有广度生成的集合特征与无oracle模型相同（Angluin条件）。2) 验证器能保证覆盖所有未见过有价值陈述同时只断言有效语句，而不可证伪语句只能转移到琐碎而非虚假。3) 核心二分法：琐碎语句有限时最优覆盖率为α/2，无限琐碎语句（即使速率趋零）最优覆盖率跳升至1-α/2，两者均为紧界。4) 两个机制均在数学的压缩模型中得到实例化。

Conclusion: 完美验证器无法替代数学品味——无限但正确的琐碎语句流不是工程事故，而是覆盖未记录有价值数学内容的必然代价。验证器将不可避免的错误从虚假重新定位到琐碎。

Abstract: AI systems coupled to proof assistants now generate formal mathematics at scale, and the gap between what a checker can verify and what a mathematician would value has become the binding constraint. We model the generation of valuable mathematics as nested language generation in the limit: a verifiable formal language $F$, accessed through a membership oracle (the proof checker), contains an unknown valuable language $H \in \mathcal{H}$ revealed only through an adversarial enumeration of a core $C \subseteq H$ of exact density $α$ (the literature). Every output is valuable ($\in H$), trivial ($\in F \setminus H$), or a hallucination ($\notin F$). We settle four questions. First, the verifier is not taste: the collections admitting generation with breadth are exactly those of the oracle-free model, characterized fiber-wise by Angluin's condition. Second, the verifier does buy sound coverage, covering all unseen valuable statements while asserting only valid ones: possible with it, impossible without it; it relocates unavoidable errors from false to trivial. Third, and centrally, a sharp dichotomy on the tight family: generators emitting finitely many trivia achieve optimal coverage $α/2$, while any infinite trivia allowance, even at vanishing rate, jumps the optimum to $1-α/2$ (both tight, for cores presented as the candidate intersection), and one generator attains both ends. The transition is in trivia count, not rate; the gap $1-α$ is the unrecorded mass. Fourth, both regimes instantiate in a compression model of mathematics. A perfect verifier cannot substitute for taste: the unbounded stream of correct-but-worthless statements is not an engineering accident but a provable necessity, since covering unrecorded valuable mathematics requires an infinite, but asymptotically negligible, stream of certified trivia.

</details>


### [76] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过残差化（residualization）方法，发现Qwen3-4B模型在代码生成前的隐藏状态中可线性解码代码正确性（AUC 0.931），但在代码修复场景中，隐藏状态中的修复成功信号完全由修复上下文（repair context）驱动，而非独立的修复理解特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型隐藏状态中是否蕴含代码正确性的可解读信息，特别是在代码生成前和代码修复失败时的两种场景下。

Method: 使用残差化工具，在444个LiveCodeBench任务上分析Qwen3-4B模型的隐藏状态。通过线性探针解码第一轮代码正确性，并通过对比方向分析研究代码修复过程中的隐藏状态变化。采用嵌套交叉验证选择探针层，并使用标签打乱的零假设进行统计显著性检验。

Result: 1）第一轮代码正确性可从提示最终隐藏状态线性解码，AUC达0.931，去除提示长度影响后仍有0.911。2）在236个修复案例中，隐藏状态变化方向虽在统计上显著，但经条件残差化控制修复上下文协变量后消失，表明该信号完全由修复上下文驱动。

Conclusion: 代码正确性在生成前即可从隐藏状态中可靠解码，但修复成功信号并非独立的隐藏状态特征，而是修复上下文的连带相关。本研究同时报告了正向与负向结果，体现了方法论的诚实性。

Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

</details>


### [77] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了PCA在保留99.9999%方差的同时，可能完全抹去关于罕见高风险事件的信号（称为"风险阴影"）。为解决这一问题，提出了Expectile PCA (ExPCA)和Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA)两种方法，通过重新加权数据协方差矩阵来关注高风险事件，理论上证明了ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息方面严格优于PCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCA通过最大化方差来降维，但方差最大化与尾部风险感知之间存在根本性错配：PCA保留的是方差，而非检测罕见灾难事件所需的信息。这种错配可能导致在保留极高比例方差的同时，完全丢失关于罕见高风险事件的关键信号，从而在高风险决策中产生严重隐患。

Method: 1) 理论证明"风险阴影"的存在：PCA可以在保留超过99.9999%方差的同时，完全抹去所有关于罕见高风险事件的信息；2) 提出ExPCA和TP-PCA两种方法，通过对数据协方差矩阵进行重新加权，使其更关注高风险事件（尾部数据）；3) 在合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明了"风险阴影"的存在——当风险阴影发生时，即使是最优分类器在PCA表示上也会退化为常数预测器。理论上证明了ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息方面严格优于PCA。合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测实验验证了上述理论主张。

Conclusion: 在高风险决策场景中，基于方差的降维方法需要被根本性重新审视。传统PCA可能在高风险场景中完全失效，而ExPCA和TP-PCA提供了更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [78] [Provably Safe, Yet Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14536)
*Kai S. Yun,Zeyang Li,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PS2-RL框架，通过两阶段架构（先训练备份策略构建隐式控制不变集，再通过可微投影层训练RL策略）实现可证明安全且可扩展的强化学习，无需显式构造不变集，在10维状态空间上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法存在两难：软约束方法缺乏形式化安全保证，而具有严格保证的方法依赖显式证书函数构造控制不变集，该过程随状态维度增长扩展性差且行为过于保守。

Method: 提出PS2-RL两阶段架构：阶段一利用安全到达值函数训练备份策略，通过前向积分系统动力学隐式生成控制不变集；阶段二通过可微投影层端到端训练RL策略，严格强制执行备份策略诱导的安全保证，同时最大化隐式不变集的体积以提升性能和可扩展性。

Result: 在最高10维的机器人控制任务上验证了框架的有效性，而此前可证明安全的RL方法在该维度下难以应用或表现不佳。

Conclusion: PS2-RL框架在保证可证明安全性的同时显著提升了可扩展性，对底层RL算法无限制，可即插即用至现有训练流程。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn policies that optimize rewards while satisfying constraints. Predominant approaches rely on soft-constrained policy optimization, which has achieved empirical success but does not provide formal safety guarantees for the learned policy. In contrast, methods with strict guarantees typically rely on explicit certificate functions, whose construction requires the direct synthesis and verification of control-invariant sets, a process that scales poorly with state dimension and often yields overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we present the Provably Safe, yet Scalable RL (PS2-RL) framework, a novel two-phase architecture for learning provably safe policies in a scalable manner, designed to overcome the key bottlenecks of prior methods. Rather than explicitly computing invariant sets, PS2-RL leverages a learned backup policy to forward-integrate the system dynamics, generating an implicit control-invariant set online. In the first phase, the backup policy is trained with our proposed safe-arrival value function, which characterizes the optimal backup policy for invariant-set construction. In the second phase, an RL policy is trained end-to-end through a differentiable projection layer that strictly enforces the safety guarantees induced by the learned backup policy. By maximizing the volume of the implicit control-invariant set in the first phase, the resulting PS2 policy from the second phase is performant and scalable, while maintaining provable safety. Crucially, PS2-RL imposes no restrictions on the underlying RL algorithm and can be plugged into any existing training pipeline. We establish theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework and evaluate it on robotic control tasks with state dimensions up to 10, a regime in which prior provably safe RL methods struggle or become impractical.

</details>


### [79] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可分解注意力局部性偏置的Transformer神经算子方法，实现了在远大于训练域的空间域上进行零样本推理（域扩展），通过结合旋转位置编码，在保持与优化注意力内核兼容的同时，显著提升了模型在更大域上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子隐式假设固定域大小，无法在推理时泛化到更大的空间域上。本文旨在解决这一域扩展问题，实现零样本推断到更大域。

Method: 提出在注意力logits计算中引入可分解的局部性偏置，实现精细可控的局部性，同时保持与查询-键内积的完全可分解性，兼容优化注意力核；结合旋转位置编码（RoPE）实现可控空间支持的可表达嵌入。

Result: 在两个PDE基准和一个3D工业大气流应用上，该方法在更大域上的零样本泛化性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过可分解的注意力局部性偏置和旋转位置编码，Transformer神经算子能够有效实现域扩展，在不改变架构的前提下大幅提升对更大空间域的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [80] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对消费级安培GPU上扩散Transformer的INT8量化后处理速度反而慢于FP8/NF4的问题，本文定位到软件层原因——INT8前向实际执行的是“量化-反量化回bf16-执行bf16矩阵乘”流程，并未调用GPU的INT8张量核心。作者通过一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM核（int8×int8→int32，在安培张量核心上运行，集成per-token×per-channel反量化和偏置，按GEMM形状自动调优）替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中的反量化到bf16路径，实现每GEMM 2.8-4.2倍加速，端到端768px加速约1.1倍（9-10%），1024px在单卡RTX 3090上156.5秒生成图像，快于单卡NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒），无质量损失。INT8从最慢变最快，1024px单GPU可行。但该优化仅适用于消费级安培，在A100和B200上不如原生bf16/FP8路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer的后训练INT8量化在消费级安培GPU上速度反而比FP8和NF4更慢，存在软件层面的虚假INT8实现问题——量化后立即反量化回bf16执行bf16矩阵乘，从未真正利用GPU的INT8张量核心，硬件计算优势完全未发挥。

Method: 设计一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM核（int8×int8→int32，在安培张量核心上运行），包含per-token×per-channel反量化和偏置融合进epilogue，按GEMM形状自动调优，替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中原来的反量化到bf16路径。

Result: 每GEMM加速2.8-4.2倍（相比bf16）；端到端768px加速约1.1倍（9-10%）；1024px单卡RTX 3090上156.5秒，快于NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒）；int8×int8→int32累积与torch._int_mm位精确，反量化输出余弦相似度1.0无NaN；无质量损失（PickScore/CLIPScore）。

Conclusion: 通过修复虚假INT8实现，INT8从最慢变为了最快量化变体，使1024px单GPU推理成为可能。但该优化明确仅适用于消费级安培GPU，在A100和B200上因原生bf16/FP8路径更快而失效。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [81] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Operational Threshold Detection and Deployable Dispatch Controller Development in Hydrogen Multi-Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14601)
*Shadi Heenatigala,Hasanika Samarasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用一年高分辨率运行数据，通过统计与机器学习框架对氢基多能源系统（H-MES）进行表征，发现太阳能辐照度解释了氢气产量45.7%的秩方差，随机森林揭示风电的非线性预测重要性，强化学习优化了氢气收益调度，核心贡献在于证明统计与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对氢基多能源系统的运行特性缺乏基于高分辨率实际数据的系统表征，统计方法与机器学习在H-MES建模中的互补性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 采用统计分析方法（秩方差分析、多元回归）与机器学习方法（随机森林、序列模型、强化学习）相结合，基于一年高分辨率运行数据对H-MES进行表征与优化。

Result: 太阳能辐照度解释氢气产量45.7%的秩方差（大效应）；电力需求呈弱逆抑制作用（ε²=0.126）；随机森林中风功率预测重要性最高（r=0.167）；序列模型利用24小时自相关（r=0.845）实现运行预测；强化学习智能体优化了氢气收益调度。

Conclusion: 统计与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中具有互补性，揭示了各自无法独立捕获的线性和非线性运行特性。

Abstract: This study presents a statistical and machine learning framework for characterizing a hydrogen-based multi-energy system (H-MES) using one year of high-resolution operational data. Statistical analysis revealed a binary operation driven by renewable surplus, with solar irradiance explaining 45.7% of rank-based variance in hydrogen production, a large effect by conventional standards. Only high-irradiance periods triggered meaningful electrolyzer engagement, while electricity demand exerted a weaker inverse suppression effect ($ε^2 = 0.126$). Multiple regression confirmed electrolyzer power as the dominant linear predictor, with a synergistic solar-wind interaction. Notably, Random Forest analysis ranked wind output first in predictive importance despite its weak bivariate correlation (r = 0.167), revealing non-linear dynamics invisible to parametric methods. A sequence model exploited strong 24-hour autocorrelation (r = 0.845) for operational forecasting, while a reinforcement learning agent optimized hydrogen revenue dispatch. The core contribution is demonstrating that statistical and machine learning approaches are complementary for H-MES modeling and control.

</details>


### [82] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化仪器分析方法，揭示了DiffusionGemma 26B模型的解码并非真正并行或块自回归，其提交顺序存在左到右的弱偏好，且"块大小"实为测量尺度的伪影；同时提出了诚实测量解码顺序的五项方法论要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放扩散语言模型被宣传为并行非自回归解码器，但实际解码顺序从未被系统测量。本文旨在填补这一实证空白，揭示模型的真实解码行为。

Method: 对DiffusionGemma 26B（基于Gemma 4的掩码离散扩散混合专家模型）的采样器接受步骤进行仪器化钩子，记录每个画布位置的提交时机、顺序和置信度。在686个提示、六种场景的探测套件上进行分析。

Result: 解码既非并行也非块自回归，而是存在从左到右的弱提交偏置；逐token看顺序很弱，随着分析粒度粗化而平滑增强；"块大小"是测量伪影而非架构属性；模型以大批量同时提交，批次内顺序本质上是未定义的；结构化JSON以任意顺序提交；数学推理中位置置信度跟踪正确性，但事实回忆中无相关信号；提交过程积极，在预算内以短时爆发完成；任务准确率与自回归Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是方法论层面的：诚实测量解码顺序必须处理尾随EOS填充、场景内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和大批次提交平局等五个问题，否则会产生虚假的解码顺序结论。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [83] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性工具热流（A-IHF），一种用于灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器。A-IHF通过图结构检测处理变量的跳跃，衰减跨跳跃的电导，并用稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。在54个合成基准测试中，受保护的A-IHF实现了最低的平均结构响应MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制函数工具变量估计器需要一阶段残差而非仅一阶段预测，高容量一阶段模型可能插值处理变量，导致残差信息不足。现有方法在处理复杂一阶段结构时面临挑战。

Method: A-IHF将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，使用预扩散检测大的处理跳跃，衰减跨跳跃的电导，并通过稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性过滤。

Result: 在54个合成基准测试中，受保护的观测A-IHF实现了最低的平均结构响应MSE；A-IHF家族在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。当图捕获分段光滑的一阶段结构时性能最强。

Conclusion: A-IHF提供了一种有效的残差提取方法，特别适用于具有分段光滑几何结构的一阶段模型，在广泛的基准测试中优于多种控制函数方法。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [84] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化中的成对探测模型，证明了即使少量带噪的成对比较查询也能显著降低最坏情况下的遗憾值，并通过方差缩减效应和连续指数权重的二阶分析给出了紧致边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将专家设置中的次线性最优查询和在线凸优化中每轮可用的成对比较反馈这两条近期研究路线统一到一个框架中，探究有限预算的成对探测能否提升全反馈OCO的 worst-case 遗憾界。

Method: 建立统一的成对探测模型，结合方差缩减效应量化探测收益，并利用连续指数权重的二阶（方差基）分析来推导遗憾上界。

Result: 当有k个δ-噪声成对探测时，遗憾界为 O(min{√(dTlnT), dTlnT/(k|1-2δ|)})，在T、k和δ上紧致（至多对数因子）。噪声参数δ从0到1/2平滑退化，当应用于有限集K的d专家预测时，所有参数（含d）完全紧致。

Conclusion: 证明了即使次线性且带噪的探测预算也能在理论上改善全反馈OCO的遗憾界，且该结果是紧致的。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [85] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限字典和预算约束的原子不确定性集选择方法，通过覆盖目标（梯度、对抗扰动等评估方向）进行数据驱动选择，证明了该目标的单调次模性，实现了(1-1/e)近似保证的贪心算法，并提供了损失界证书和半径校准规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习与优化依赖于不确定性模型的选择，现有方法缺乏系统的准则来决定模型应覆盖哪些不确定性方向。

Method: 提出基于覆盖目标的原子不确定性集数据驱动选择规则，证明目标函数单调且次模，采用贪心方法实现(1-1/e)近似保证，并给出损失界证书和半径校准规则。

Result: 证明了选择规则的目标函数具有单调次模性，贪心方法达到(1-1/e)近似保证，且存在匹配的硬度障碍；提供了经验损失界证书和样本外控制的半径校准。

Conclusion: 原子不确定性集的选择问题可通过次模优化有效求解，理论保障与实际校准规则相结合，为鲁棒优化中不确定性建模提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [86] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了结合图基因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒逼近的自适应通用策略，用于在互联数据中高效识别最优结构，具有亚线性时间复杂度和线性数据量复杂度，并在合成与真实交通数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模互联数据中的最优结构识别需要大量采样和计算资源，而现有的非线性统计关系处理方式复杂度高，亟需更高效、鲁棒的通用策略。

Method: 开发了基于图因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法以及泰勒逼近功能过程的通用自适应策略，建立了亚线性时间复杂度和线性数据量复杂度的理论性能保证，并分析了噪声干扰、模型收敛和解空间不匹配等不确定性下的鲁棒性。

Result: 建立了亚线性时间、线性数据量的理论保证，框架对多种不确定性具有鲁棒性，且只需极少的先验假设或条件，在合成数据和真实交通数据集上均验证了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架在理论上具备高效性和鲁棒性，在实践中通过多种改进可拓展至特定或扩展场景，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过88个eGeMAPS声学特征和线性/非线性回归探针，揭示了生物声学预训练模型编码的语音类似特征。发现无单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间，拼接嵌入表现最佳；响度特征最易恢复(R²=0.76)，基频最难恢复(R²=0.33)；通过交叉引用可恢复性与特征显著性，提供了数据驱动的模型选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物声学预训练音频嵌入缺乏透明度，不清楚模型编码了哪些声学特征以及哪些特征对特定任务有用，限制了模型在稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展应用。

Method: 使用88维eGeMAPS特征集，涵盖六个分类群，采用线性与非线性回归探针量化每个模型捕获的声学属性。通过交叉引用特征可恢复性(R²)与每物种特征显著性(NMI)来推导模型选择指导。

Result: 无免费午餐模式：无单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间。拼接嵌入达到最高性能，表明模型间存在互补的声学空间覆盖。响度特征编码最好(R²=0.76)，F0最难恢复(R²=0.33)。

Conclusion: 通过分析模型编码的声学特征可恢复性及其与物种特征显著性的关系，可为生物声学任务提供数据驱动的模型选择依据。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [88] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路由专用的双适配器知识编辑方法（\method{}），通过相关性路由器决定是否注入编辑记忆，并分别使用编辑适配器和局部性适配器处理不同路由路径，在三个基准测试上取得了最优的概率偏好准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要更新选定事实的同时保持无关行为不变，核心设计问题不仅是如何写入编辑，还包括何时抑制编辑。

Method: 提出\method{}方法，包含一个相关性路由器（lexical neural router或BGE embedding routing）决定提示是否应接收编辑记忆；路由后的提示使用编辑适配器（偏好新对象），非直接提示使用局部性适配器（保留或恢复原对象偏好）。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，\method{}在CF（0.8180）、ZSRE（0.8946）和MQuAKE（0.9922）三个基准上取得最佳概率偏好准确率，在Qwen3-8B上也有相同趋势。组件消融实验表明增益主要来自编辑注入与离路抑制的分离而非单纯增加LoRA容量。

Conclusion: 不同数据集的最佳路由边界不同：CF上lexical neural router最安全，ZSRE和MQuAKE上BGE embedding路由更优。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [89] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明，Braun等人提出的CC玩具模型并非叠加计算（computation in superposition）的合适范例。研究发现，CC模型通过噪声残差流混合输入，对应于标签中的非预期混合矩阵。模型性能提升与混合矩阵的幅度相关，当移除混合矩阵时性能消失。学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间，表明混合项主导了模型解。基于混合矩阵的SNMF基线可复现定性损失曲线，但无法匹配训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证CC玩具模型（Braun et al., 2025）是否为叠加计算（computation in superposition）的实例。CC模型声称用50个神经元计算100个ReLU函数，损失优于仅表示50个ReLU函数的预期，这一现象值得深入探究其机制。

Method: 1. 分析CC模型的噪声残差流如何混合输入，发现对应标签中的非预期混合矩阵；2. 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项，分别考察其贡献；3. 分析神经元方向在混合矩阵特征值子空间中的分布；4. 提出基于混合矩阵的SNMF基线进行对比实验。

Result: 1. 模型性能提升与混合矩阵幅度正相关，移除混合矩阵后性能消失；2. 学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间；3. SNMF基线可复现定性损失曲线且优于先前基线，但无法完全匹配训练模型。

Conclusion: CC模型不适合作为叠加计算的玩具模型，其表现出的"超能力"实际上源于输入混合机制而非真正的叠加计算。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [90] [Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14679)
*Anthony Pineci,Yunzong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在线库存优化中，通过维护隐藏目标并结合投影操作的原则是最优的，可将遗憾界从反比例改进为反平方根依赖，并首次给出强凸损失的 polylogarithmic 遗憾界和动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线库存优化中，库存结转使得可行动作集依赖于历史决策，导致状态依赖问题。现有方法只在单一线性容量约束下有效，缺乏对一般凸容量集的通用最优方法。

Method: 提出隐藏目标维护与投影操作的原则：用在线学习器维护一个隐藏目标，并将其投影到当前可行的订货-补货集上。引入范数对齐原理，将状态变量定义为隐藏目标到可行集的距离（与投影使用相同的范数），将高维问题约化为标量队列控制。

Result: 1) 该方法在一般有界凸容量集上是最优的；2) 将遗憾界从逆比例依赖改进为反平方根依赖，并给出匹配下界；3) 首次给出强凸损失的 polylogarithmic 遗憾界；4) 首次给出适应欧几里得路径变化的动态遗憾界。

Conclusion: 范数对齐下的隐藏目标-投影原则是解决在线库存优化问题的一般性最优框架，通过将多维问题约化为一维队列控制，突破了以往乘积方法的局限性。实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets.
  The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.

</details>


### [91] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多组均值估计d臂赌博机中基于最大风险目标的主动学习问题，提出了首个适用于任意有限方差假设类的局部极小极大下界，并引入了方差局部曲率（VLC）这一复杂性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习算法在多组均值估计中缺乏对最坏情况不确定性（最大风险）的系统性理论下界，无法精确刻画问题难度的影响因素。

Method: 采用局部极小极大框架，引入损失诱导的ℓ1决策空间几何和基于表示的实例生成器，将困难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算。

Result: 推导了首个通用下界，将困难分解为预算项、异方差性指标和方差局部曲率（VLC）三个正交因子；对于光滑类，VLC可重参数化为方差-费雪信息量；与最强上界对比表明在广泛范围内近最优（对数因子内），在高度异质实例中存在系统性差距。

Conclusion: 本文建立了多组均值估计最大风险目标的极小极大理论，VLC作为模型依赖性复杂性度量具有闭式解，为理解主动学习中的异方差性影响提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


### [92] [Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14695)
*Jinsu Kim,Jihoon Tack,Noah Lee,Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从单一描述中提取角色特定子网络，构建轻量级角色扮演模型，在保持角色扮演性能的同时大幅降低计算开销。实验表明该方法比现有SOTA剪枝技术在角色扮演性能保持上提升达93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型作为角色扮演聊天机器人时，为每个角色配备完整的通用大模型导致计算成本过高，且直接剪枝会无法区分冗余知识与角色关键特征，导致角色扮演性能严重下降。

Method: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，从单一角色描述中分离出角色特定的子网络（sub-networks），通过隔离角色专属参数来实现轻量化角色扮演模型。

Result: 在RoleBench上，Persona-Pruner相比最强基线将角色扮演性能下降（相对于稠密模型）减少了93.8%（以LLM-as-a-judge分数衡量），同时保持了通用LLM能力。

Conclusion: 角色身份仅依赖于模型总容量的一小部分，通过针对性提取角色子网络可以有效实现轻量化角色扮演模型，同时显著优于现有通用剪枝方法。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Personalization and Evaluation of Conversational Information Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13717)
*Hideaki Joko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本论文围绕构建可靠且融入个人情境的对话式信息访问（CIA）系统，研究了三个核心挑战：对话中的人物实体链接、个性化回复生成，以及可解释的系统自动评估方法，并分别提出了CREL、LAPS和FACE三种解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越倾向于在对话交互中直接获取答案而非传统超链接，现有信息检索系统缺乏对个人情境（personal context）的建模，无法生成真正个性化的回复，且缺乏有效的自动评估方法。

Method: (1) 针对个人情境提取：定义对话实体链接（ConEL）任务，构建相应数据集，并提出适用于对话场景的实体链接方法CREL；(2) 针对个性化回复生成：提出LAPS方法高效构建大规模人工撰写个性化对话数据集，研究如何利用用户偏好生成个性化回复；(3) 针对系统评估：提出FACE自动无参考评估方法，评估完整对话并保持与人类判断高度一致。

Result: 构建了对话实体链接数据集，提出了有效的对话实体链接方法CREL；通过LAPS方法获得了大规模个性化对话数据集；FACE自动评估方法与人类判断高度对齐。

Conclusion: 论文系统性地解决了构建个性化对话式信息访问系统所面临的三个关键挑战，在实体链接、个性化回复生成和系统评估方面均提出了有效的解决方案，为构建可靠、个性化的CIA系统奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: Conversational interactions have reshaped information retrieval systems, as users increasingly favour direct answers over traditional hyperlinks. To build reliable Conversational Information Access (CIA) systems that account for personal context, this thesis addresses challenges: (1) personal context extraction, (2) personalized response generation, and (3) effective and interpretable system evaluation. First, we tackle personal context extraction by studying what Entity Linking (EL) in conversations entails, introducing a dataset for conversational entity linking (ConEL), and proposing CREL, a novel EL method tailored for conversational settings. Second, we focus on personalized response generation by proposing LAPS, a method for efficiently constructing large-scale, human-written, personalized conversational datasets, and using them to study how users' preferences can be utilized to generate personalized responses. Finally, we address the need for effective and interpretable system evaluation by introducing FACE, an automatic, reference-free method that assesses entire conversations and aligns closely with human judgments.

</details>


### [94] [Nomenclature Ontology for Medical And Disease names (NOMAD): taxonomy of types and origins of disease names](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13719)
*Spiros Denaxas,Cai Ytsma,Giannos Louloudis,Jackie MacArthur,Harry Hemingway*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了NOMAD（医学与疾病名称命名法本体），一个对疾病命名惯例进行系统分类的元分类体系。研究基于ICD-10-CM 2026字母索引的22,548个条目，采用机器学习驱动的三阶段分类流程，建立了包含9个顶层类别和20个子类别的两级分类法。结果显示解剖学类别最普遍（63.8%），描述性（48.4%）和病理生理学（40.2%）次之，而以人名和地理命名的疾病占比低于文化预期（分别为9.7%和1.9%），其中女性人名命名的疾病仅占2.6%。人工验证准确率70%，部分一致率26%（Cohen's Kappa 0.832）。不同ICD章节的命名惯例差异显著，反映了各专科的认知传统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疾病命名历经数个世纪自然演化，使用希腊语、拉丁语和阿拉伯语术语，反映了不同医学发现时代的特征。尽管存在明显的命名实践异质性，但目前缺乏对所有疾病命名惯例进行系统描述的分类框架。

Method: 开发了NOMAD元分类体系，包含9个顶层类别和20个子类别；应用于ICD-10-CM 2026字母索引的22,548个条目；采用三阶段机器学习驱动分类流程；多标签分类以反映医学命名法的组合性质；对10%样本进行人工验证。

Result: 分类覆盖99.1%的术语，平均每条目2.12个标签。解剖学类别最普遍（63.8%），描述性（48.4%）和病理生理学（40.2%）次之；人名和地理标签分别为9.7%和1.9%；女性人名疾病仅占2.6%。人工验证完全一致率70%，部分一致率26%（Cohen's Kappa 0.832）。不同ICD章节命名惯例差异显著。

Conclusion: NOMAD提供了一个对疾病命名惯例进行系统分类的框架，揭示了不同疾病领域命名传统的显著差异，反映了专科特定的认识论传统。其中以人名和地理命名的疾病占比低于文化预期，且女性人名命名的疾病比例极低，反映了命名实践中的性别不平等。

Abstract: The nomenclature of human disease has developed organically over the past centuries using Greek, Latin, and Arabic terminology and reflects the idiosyncrasies of different eras of medical discovery. Despite evident heterogeneity in naming practices, no systematic framework exists for characterising these conventions across all diseases. In this paper, we describe the Nomenclature Ontology for Medical And Disease names (NOMAD), a meta-taxonomy that classifies disease names according to their naming conventions. We developed a two-level taxonomy comprising 9 top-level categories and 20 subcategories and applied it to 22,548 index entries from the ICD-10-CM 2026 Alphabetical Index in a scalable three-stage machine learning-driven classification pipeline. Classification was multi-label, reflecting the compositional nature of medical nomenclature. We classified 99.1% of terms with a mean of 2.12 labels per entry. Anatomical categories were the most prevalent (63.8% of entries), followed by Descriptive (48.4%) and Pathophysiological (40.2%), while Eponymous and Geographical labels were less common than their cultural prominence might suggest (9.7% and 1.9% respectively). Among all Eponymous diseases, we identified only 57 (2.6%) of diseases named after a female person. We manually reviewed a random sample of n=2,255 entries (10%) for accuracy and calculated a full agreement rate of 70% and partial agreement rate of 26% (macro-averaged Cohen's Kappa score 0.832). Naming convention profiles varied substantially across ICD-10-CM chapters, reflecting specialty-specific epistemological traditions: infectious disease chapters were dominated by etiological labels and showed the highest proportion of geographical region related labels, the circulatory chapter by anatomical and pathophysiological labels, and mental and behavioural disorders showed the highest prevalence of socio-behavioral labels.

</details>


### [95] [TASR: Training-Free Adaptive Stopping for Iterative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13814)
*Adrian Kieback,Uyiosa Philip Amadasun,Aman Chadha,Aaron Elkins*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TASR（无训练自适应停止规则），一种无需训练的、单行谓词的迭代检索增强生成停止策略，基于回答重复和logit边际进行决策，在多种配置下以更少调用达到或超过固定检索步数的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代检索增强生成智能体常在模型已收敛于答案后继续检索，浪费不必要的调用。现有方法需要从带标签轨迹中学习停止策略，且需为每个新模型或任务重新训练，缺乏通用性和高效性。

Method: 提出TASR，核心为单行谓词：当模型重复上一轮归一化答案且等渗校准后的logit边际超过0.25时触发停止。不学习任何分类器或价值头，阈值在所有24种（模型、检索器、语料库）配置上固定。规则从381个候选停止规则的穷举枚举中选出。

Result: 在3模型×2数据集的干扰物网格上，TASR以62.6%的调用量保留固定k=5的94.8%宏F1，超过固定k=3的F1 +3.42。在开放域BM25和密集检索场景中均表现一致，无显著性能退化。信号质量分析显示，RLHF调优模型的口头置信度几乎坍塌（96.5%为5，熵0.182 nats），而logit边际的类条件分离度高出44倍。

Conclusion: TASR是一种可审计、无训练的Pareto基线，作为学习型停止控制器的比较基准。代码已公开。

Abstract: Iterative retrieval-augmented generation agents commonly overspend by continuing to retrieve after the model has converged on an answer, incurring calls that change neither the prediction nor the supporting evidence. Existing remedies learn a stopping policy from labeled trajectories, tying the decision to a trained component that requires retraining for each new model or task. We propose TASR (Training-Free Adaptive Stopping Rule), a one-line predicate that fires when the model repeats its previous-round normalized answer and the isotonically calibrated logit margin exceeds 0.25. No classifier or value head is learned; the threshold is fixed across all twenty-four (model, retriever, corpus) configurations we evaluate. On a 3-model x 2-dataset distractor grid, TASR retains 94.8% of fixed-k=5's macro F1 at 62.6% of its calls and exceeds fixed-k=3 by +3.42 F1. The pattern holds on nine open-domain BM25 cells (55.01 F1 at 2.98 calls vs. 54.33 at 3.00 for fixed-k=3) and, with calibration locked from the distractor split, on nine dense-retrieval cells across two retriever families, with zero significant regressions in either extension. The rule was selected from an exhaustive enumeration of 381 candidate stopping rules; no alternative Pareto-dominates it on any evaluated configuration. A signal-quality analysis shows that verbalized 1-5 confidence collapses on RLHF-tuned models (96.5% of values equal 5, entropy 0.182 nats), while the logit margin achieves 44x better class-conditional separation, grounding the design in a measurable model pathology. TASR is an auditable, training-free Pareto baseline against which learned stopping controllers can be compared. Code is publicly available.

</details>


### [96] [Hybrid Neural Retrieval with Generative Query Refinement for Quranic Passage Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13837)
*Mohamed G. Salman,Mohammad E. Moftah,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四阶段神经架构用于《古兰经》多节文检索，结合混合候选检索（AraColBERT密集索引+BM25稀疏检索）、语义重排序（CAMeLBERTmix交叉编码器）、置信门控过滤零答案查询以及AraT5多节聚合模块，在扩展的Quran QA 2022数据集上Recall@10达0.7024，MAP@10达0.4947。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《古兰经》经文检索面临两大挑战：一是查询所用的现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）与经文所用的古典阿拉伯语（CA）之间存在语义鸿沟；二是传统检索方法难以处理多节文检索和零答案查询过滤问题。

Method: 提出四阶段神经架构：第一阶段采用混合候选检索（AraColBERT密集索引+BM25稀疏检索）；第二阶段使用CAMeLBERTmix交叉编码器进行语义重排序；第三阶段应用置信门控机制过滤零答案查询；第四阶段基于AraT5实现多节文聚合优化。

Result: 在扩展的Quran QA 2022数据集上，Recall@10达到0.7024，MAP@10达到0.4947。尽管在绝对顶部排名精度（MRR=0.5807）上相比高度优化的单一模型略有折衷，但整体架构在多节文检索方面更为全面、可靠且具备上下文感知能力。

Conclusion: 该四阶段神经架构有效解决了MSA与CA之间的语义鸿沟问题，在提升多节文检索召回率和精度的同时，通过置信门控机制有效过滤了零答案查询，为《古兰经》经文检索提供了更全面、可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Quranic Passage Retrieval (PR) could be a challenging task due to the linguistic complexity and the semantic gap between the Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) used in daily queries and the Classical Arabic (CA) of the Holy Quran. These factors hinder conventional retrieval methods. To handle these limitations and improve multi-verse retrieval and filter the zero-answer queries, this paper proposes a four-phase neural architecture designed to enhance retrieval accuracy and contextual understanding. The methodology combines hybrid candidate retrieval using AraColBERT dense indexing and BM25 sparse retrieval, followed by semantic reranking with a CAMeLBERTmix cross-encoder. A confidence gating mechanism is then applied to filter zero-answer queries, and an AraT5-based refinement module for multi-verse aggregation. The system is evaluated on an expanded version of the Quran QA 2022 dataset. Results show improved performance compared to the baseline models, achieving a Recall@10 of 0.7024 and a Mean Average Precision (MAP@10) of 0.4947. While the system exhibits a marginal tradeoff in absolute top-rank precision (MRR = 0.5807) compared to heavily optimised single models, the proposed architecture provides a substantially more comprehensive, reliable, and context aware solution for multi-verse Quranic passage retrieval.

</details>


### [97] [Mood-Aware Music Recommendation: Integrating User Affective Signals into Ranking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13858)
*Terence Zeng,Abhishek K. Umrawal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于情绪条件的排序框架，通过将用户情感信号（能量-效价空间）整合到音乐推荐系统中，以改善传统协同过滤在稀疏交互数据下的表现。实验表明，该方法能提升用户感知的推荐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐推荐系统在用户-物品交互稀疏的领域中表现不佳，而内容过滤方法虽然考虑了流派、乐器等特征，但对情绪识别这一影响用户音乐选择的重要因素关注不足。

Method: 提出了一种情绪条件排序框架，通过softmax采样在能量-效价空间中整合用户情感信号，将其融入推荐流程。

Result: 单盲实验结果显示，与基线系统相比，用户对所提系统的推荐质量感知有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将基于情绪的信号融入音乐推荐是有效的，为利用情感信息改善个性化推荐提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Recommendation systems are essential in modern music streaming platforms due to the vast amount of available content. While collaborative filtering is widely used to suggest items based on the preferences of others with similar patterns, it performs poorly in domains where user-item interactions are sparse, such as music. Content-based filtering is an alternative approach that examines the qualities of the items themselves. Genre, instrumentation, and lyrics have been explored; however, relatively little attention has been given to emotion recognition. Since a user's emotional state strongly influences their music choice, incorporating mood signals offers a promising direction for personalization. In this work, we propose a mood-conditioned ranking framework that integrates user affective signals into the recommendation process via softmax-based sampling in the energy-valence space. We evaluate the approach via single-blind experiments in which participants compare recommendations from the proposed system against a baseline. The results indicate improved perceived recommendation quality, providing preliminary evidence for the effectiveness of incorporating mood-based inputs into music recommendations.

</details>


### [98] [ADORE: Iterative Query Expansion with Retrieval-Grounded Relevance Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13905)
*Amin Bigdeli,Negar Arabzadeh,Radin Hamidi Rad,Sajad Ebrahimi,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ADORE是一个迭代式查询扩展框架，通过将检索结果转化为反馈信号（识别需强化、未覆盖和需抑制的内容），显著优于现有方法，在BEIR和BRIGHT上nDCG@10分别提升3.6%和9.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的查询扩展方法多为生成驱动，仅依赖单次生成或未经验证的迭代，未利用目标语料库的实际检索反馈，导致检索漂移、误导性词汇放大以及难以区分相关与不相关文档的关键词缺失。

Method: ADORE框架包含三个核心组件：LLM生成伪段落、检索器暴露语料库响应、相关性评估器评估检索文档与原始查询的相关性。通过多轮迭代，将检索结果转化为反馈信号，识别需要强化、补充覆盖和抑制的内容，指导下一轮扩展。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning、BEIR和BRIGHT数据集上，ADORE一致优于强基线。在BEIR上，相比BM25平均提升24.5% nDCG@10，相比最强查询扩展方法提升3.6%；在BRIGHT上，相比BM25提升122.9%，相比最佳查询扩展基线提升9.2%。

Conclusion: 有效的查询扩展需要基于检索结果的反馈，而非仅依赖单次生成或未经验证的迭代；ADORE框架通过将检索结果转化为可操作的反馈信号，显著提升了检索性能。

Abstract: LLM-based query expansion improves retrieval by enriching the original query with additional context. Yet most methods remain generation-driven, producing plausible pseudo-documents or expansions without checking how the target corpus responds. This can introduce retrieval drift, amplify misleading vocabulary, or miss terms that distinguish relevant from non-relevant documents. We argue that effective expansion requires retrieval-grounded feedback, not just single-pass generation or unverified iteration. We introduce ADORE (ADapt, Observe, Relevance Evaluate), an iterative framework that turns retrieval outcomes into feedback for the next expansion. At each round, an LLM generates pseudo-passages, a retriever exposes the corpus response, and a relevance assessor evaluates retrieved documents against the original query. These judgments identify what to reinforce, what remains undercovered, and what to suppress. Across TREC Deep Learning, BEIR, and BRIGHT, ADORE consistently outperforms strong query expansion baselines with notable improvements across nearly all evaluation settings, improving average nDCG@10 by 24.5% over BM25 and 3.6% over the strongest prior query expansion method on BEIR, and by 122.9% over BM25 and 9.2% over the best query expansion baseline on BRIGHT. Our code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [99] [When Recommendation Denoising Meets Popularity Bias: Understanding and Mitigating Their Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14046)
*Guohang Zeng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了去噪推荐中的小损失启发式会加剧流行度偏差，因为尾部正样本因稀疏性天然具有较大损失，导致被误降权。作者提出流行度感知去噪（PAD）框架，对高曝光项强去噪、对长尾项保守处理，实验证明该方法能提升准确率-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪推荐方法依赖小损失启发式来识别噪声交互（如误点击），但该启发式未考虑流行度偏差：尾部（长尾）正样本因观测稀疏而拟合困难、损失较大，即使反映真实偏好也可能被错误降权，加剧了头部-尾部信号不平衡。

Method: 形式化定义了去噪权重下有效头尾信号比，并推导了条件重分配结果：当尾部正样本损失分布相对头部右移时，小损失重权会提高有效头尾信号比。据此提出PAD，一个轻量级即插即用框架，根据物品流行度调节去噪强度——对高曝光项加强去噪，对尾部项保持保守，保留更多干净但难拟合的长尾信号。

Result: 在三个数据集和三种骨干模型上的实验表明，PAD普遍优于代表性的去噪基线方法，尤其在MF类推荐器上实现了更优的准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 去噪推荐需要意识到流行度偏差的影响，简单的基于损失大小的降权会系统性抑制长尾正样本。PAD通过流行度感知的调节机制，在保持去噪效果的同时更好地保护尾部信号，实现更公平有效的推荐。

Abstract: Implicit feedback is the dominant data source for recommender systems, but behavioral logs are often contaminated by false-positive interactions caused by mis-clicks, biased exposure, and interface effects. Denoising recommendation methods improve robustness by down-weighting or filtering interactions suspected to be noisy, often relying on the small-loss heuristic. We revisit this heuristic through the lens of popularity bias. Tail-item positives can be harder to fit because they are sparsely observed, and thus may receive larger losses even when they reflect genuine user preference. Under such popularity-dependent loss patterns, monotone loss-based reweighting can suppress clean-but-hard tail signals and increase the head-tail imbalance in effective supervision.
  We formalize this interaction through the effective head-tail signal ratio induced by denoising weights and derive a conditional reallocation result: when the loss distribution of tail positives is right-shifted relative to that of head positives, small-loss reweighting increases the effective head-tail signal ratio compared with ERM. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Popularity-Aware Denoising (PAD), a lightweight plug-in framework that modulates denoising strength by item popularity. PAD applies stronger denoising to highly exposed items while being more conservative on tail items, preserving more clean-but-hard long-tail signals. Experiments on three datasets and three backbones show that PAD generally improves over representative denoising baselines and provides favorable accuracy-diversity tradeoffs, especially on MF-style recommenders.

</details>


### [100] [Knowledge Graph Enhanced Memory-Augmented Retrieval for Long Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14047)
*Ghadir Alselwi,Basem Suleiman,Hao Xue,Shoaib Jameel,Hakim Hacid,Flora D. Salim,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KGERMAR通过动态构建上下文知识图谱增强长文本语言模型的记忆能力，结合语义相似性和实体关系进行检索，在多个长文本基准上实现了更低的困惑度和更高的记忆效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言建模不仅需要扩展上下文窗口，还需在数千token中保持对实体状态和关系的连贯理解，而纯语义相似度方法无法解决这一挑战。

Method: KGERMAR在推理过程中动态构建上下文特定的知识图谱，通过实时实体和关系抽取构建图谱，并将图结构嵌入与文本语义通过多组件记忆架构融合，维护上下文、语义和结构三个记忆库，通过学习权重融合检索信号。

Result: 在SlimPajama、WikiText-103、PG-19和Proof-pile上，KGERMAR在1K到32K上下文长度下困惑度降低高达8.5%，记忆效率提升2-2.5倍，在五个NLU任务上上下文学习表现更优。

Conclusion: 动态知识图谱构建方法通过适应输入上下文的领域特定知识表示，推动了记忆增强语言建模的发展，优于依赖固定知识库的方法。

Abstract: Long-context language modeling requires not only extending context windows but maintaining coherent understanding of entity states and relationships across thousands of tokens -- a challenge that semantic similarity alone cannot address. KGERMAR addresses this by constructing dynamic, context-specific knowledge graphs from input text during inference, enabling domain-adaptive retrieval that leverages both semantic similarity and explicit entity relationships. The framework performs real-time entity and relation extraction to build contextual knowledge graphs, then integrates graph-structural embeddings with textual semantics through a multi-component memory architecture. Three memory banks -- contextual, semantic, and structural -- are maintained with retrieval signals fused via learned weights to capture both surface-level semantics and deeper relational patterns. Evaluated on SlimPajama (84.7K training examples), WikiText-103 (4,358 examples), PG-19 (100 examples), and Proof-pile (46.3K examples), KGERMAR achieves up to 8.5\% lower perplexity and 2--2.5x better memory efficiency than memory-augmented baselines across context lengths from 1K to 32K tokens, with superior in-context learning performance across five NLU tasks. The dynamic knowledge graph construction approach advances memory-augmented language modeling by enabling domain-specific knowledge representation that adapts to input contexts rather than relying on fixed knowledge bases.

</details>


### [101] [CoRe: A Continuously Reward-Finetuned LLM Query Rewriter for Multi-Stage Context-Aware Relevance in Web-Scale Video Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14127)
*Yilin Wen,Rong Yang,Xiaojia Chang,Hong Sun,Gefu Tang,Chunhui Liu,Jeffrey Chen,Zeyu Ma,Lisong Qiu,Xiaochuan Fan,Congjia Yu,Quan Zhou,Yuheng Chen,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了CoRe系统，一种基于混合偏好优化的在线查询重写器，通过多模态相关性模型作为奖励源、半在线训练循环和自动防护门机制，在短视频搜索引擎中实现每周重新部署，显著降低了变更查询率并提升了相关性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中基于LLM的查询重写器面临训练奖励与排序器对齐但训练成本需低廉以支持持续部署的矛盾，现有离线奖励代理存在仿真-生产差距。

Method: 1) 使用部署的多模态相关性模型作为奖励源，采用乘法比率形式对齐生产融合代数；2) 半在线混合偏好优化循环（MPO）：DPO风格的成对目标仅对top-k/bottom-k子集梯度回传，阶段性结构减少参数同步频率；3) 自动防护门检测并恢复奖励攻击；4) 重写器输出作为并行相关性信号在召回、粗排、精排阶段使用，不替代原始信号。

Result: 在短视频搜索引擎中连续五个月每周重新部署，两次在线A/B实验（先在精排部署，再扩展至召回和粗排）显示：在受重写影响的查询上，变更查询率显著降低，所有主要相关性和用户参与度指标均朝预期方向变化。

Conclusion: CoRe通过奖励设计、高效训练循环和安全机制，弥合了离线奖励代理与生产对齐之间的差距，实现了低成本、可持续部署的查询重写系统。

Abstract: LLM-based query rewriters in production face a tension: the training reward must reflect how the rewrite is consumed by the production ranker, yet the training procedure must be cheap enough to support continuous redeployment as data drifts. We present CoRe (Context Relevance), such a system, redeployed weekly for over five months in a major short-video search engine. Our reward uses the deployed multimodal relevance model as its source and a multiplicative ratio form mirroring the production fusion algebra, closing the simulation-production gap that offline reward proxies leave open. A semi-online Mixed Preference Optimization loop makes this reward affordable at multi-million-instance weekly scale: a DPO-style pairwise objective restricts the gradient pass to a small top-k/bottom-k subset of sampled trajectories, and a phase structure reduces trainer/inference-server parameter syncs from per-step to per-phase. An automated promotion gate over reward-like and stability metrics detected and recovered from a real reward-hacking incident in production. Rewriter output is consumed as parallel relevance signals at recall, rawrank, and finerank without displacing the original signals, bounding rewriter-failure blast radius. Online A/B from two sequential production launches, first deploying the rewriter at finerank, then extending consumption to recall and rawrank, delivers statistically significant reductions in change-query rate on rewrite-impacted queries, with all headline relevance and engagement metrics moving in the expected direction.

</details>


### [102] [ChronoID: Infusing Explicit Temporal Signals into Semantic IDs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14260)
*Dongdong Nian,Dongqi Fu,Chenliang Xu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Jian Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoID框架，通过将时间信号显式融入语义ID学习来解决生成式推荐中时间信息缺失的问题，并构建了时间显式推荐基准来验证方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐中的语义ID完全忽略时间信息，隐式地假设物品语义和用户意图在时间上是平稳的，这与真实推荐场景中交互节奏不断演化的实际情况不符。

Method: 系统性地沿三个正交维度（时间信号）刻画设计空间，提出统一框架ChronoID用于时间感知的语义ID学习，并构建时间显式的生成推荐基准。

Result: 回答了三个关键问题：有效的时间注入方式、架构设计方法、性能增益来源。

Conclusion: 显式地将时间信息融入语义ID能够更准确地建模用户与物品随时间演化的交互模式，从而提升生成式推荐的性能。

Abstract: Semantic IDs are crucial in generative recommendation, but with a fundamental limitation: temporal information is not well incorporated into semantic IDs. Instead, time influences recommendation only implicitly (e.g., through session construction heuristics, preference alignment, or sequence order), while existing semantic ID learning remains entirely time-agnostic. This design conflates interactions occurring under distinct temporal contexts into identical semantic representations, implicitly assuming that item semantics and user intent are temporally stationary. Such an assumption is misaligned with real-world recommendation scenarios, where evolving interaction rhythms play a central role. In this work, we investigate where and how the explicit time should be incorporated into semantic ID for generative recommendation. First, we systematically characterize the design space along three orthogonal dimensions of temporal signals and present a unified framework, ChronoID, for time-aware semantic ID learning. Then, by contributing a new time-explicit generation recommendation benchmark, ChronoID answers the questions: what is the effective way of infusing time, how to design the architecture, and where does the gain come from.

</details>


### [103] [ScoreGate: Adaptive Chunk Selection for Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Dual-Score Statistical Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14269)
*Karamvir Singh,Arvind Jain*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ScoreGate是一种轻量级得分空间决策机制，利用双编码器相似度和交叉编码器重排序分数，在推理时自适应控制检索基数，无需额外模型调用。它在MS MARCO上以减少35%的检索块实现MRR@10=0.401，在内部基准上达到零误报、97.77-99.34%召回率，同时减少34.8%的每查询token数，仅增加31ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定基数检索（top-K）无法适应查询复杂度差异，狭窄查询会过度检索，组合查询则检索不足，导致效率与质量的双重损失。

Method: ScoreGate利用标准流水线中已有的两个分数——双编码器相似度s_i和交叉编码器重排序分数r_i，构建得分空间决策机制。核心洞察在于交叉编码器的确认能力可以"拯救"因词汇不匹配而被双编码器低排名的语义相关块，这是固定K或单分数阈值方法无法解决的模式。

Result: 在MS MARCO（200条开发查询）上，MRR@10=0.401，检索块减少35%；内部基准（n=300, Fleiss' kappa=0.87）上零误报（95% CI [96.4%, 100%]），召回率97.77-99.34%，每查询token减少34.8%，仅增加31ms延迟。

Conclusion: 自适应检索基数能够在不降低检索质量的前提下提升检索效率，MS MARCO和真实生产流量均验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Fixed-cardinality retrieval injects a constant top-K chunks into the generator regardless of query complexity, causing over-retrieval for narrow queries and under-retrieval for compositional ones. We describe ScoreGate, a lightweight score-space decision mechanism that controls retrieval cardinality at inference time using two scores already produced by the standard pipeline: bi-encoder similarity s_i and cross-encoder reranker score r_i, with no additional model inference calls required. Its core insight is that cross-encoder affirmation can rescue semantically relevant chunks that bi-encoder retrieval ranks poorly due to vocabulary mismatch -- a failure mode unaddressed by fixed-K or single-score thresholding. On MS MARCO (200 dev queries), ScoreGate achieves MRR@10 = 0.401 with 35% fewer retained chunks than Standard Top-K. On an internal benchmark (n=300, Fleiss' kappa=0.87), ScoreGate observed zero false positives (95% CI [96.4%, 100%]) at 97.77-99.34% recall, with 34.8% fewer tokens per query and only 31ms added latency. Results on both MS MARCO and real-world production traffic suggest that adaptive retrieval cardinality can improve retrieval efficiency without degrading retrieval quality.

</details>


### [104] [Verifiable User Simulation for Search and Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14474)
*Chenglong Ma,Xinye Wanyan,Danula Hettiachchi,Ziqi Xu,Yongli Ren,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该教程提出了一种可审计的LLM用户模拟器设计框架，包含七个可审计组件（Persona、Contract、Execution、Trace、Verification、Feedback、Refinement），并通过动手实验教授如何对模拟器进行保真度、可信度和人口统计偏差检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户模拟器存在不透明性问题，难以解释模拟用户为何做出特定选择，且研究表明LLM可能根据用户背景特征（语言、教育水平、文化背景）产生歧视性响应，引发对少数群体公平对待的担忧。

Method: 提出一个设计与审计框架，将用户模拟器视为包含七个可审计组件的可验证工程制品：结构化Persona、任务感知Contract、人机匹配Execution、可审计Trace、与Persona对齐的Verification、结构化Feedback，以及更新Persona和Contract的Refinement循环。

Result: 通过两个动手实验（推荐列表评估和搜索查询构建），参与者将学习端到端检查模拟器行为，区分诊断性差异分析与统计验证，并应用保真度、可信度和人口统计偏差检查。

Conclusion: 该教程面向信息检索和推荐系统领域的研究者与实践者，旨在推动用户行为模拟领域的可解释性和负责任AI实践。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) based user simulation is increasingly adopted for evaluating search engines, recommender systems, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines, yet most simulators remain opaque: it is difficult to determine why a simulated user made a particular choice or whether that choice is consistent with the intended user profile. Compounding this, recent research shows that LLMs can produce biased or discriminatory responses depending on user background characteristics such as language, education level, and cultural context, raising concerns about the equitable treatment of minority and disadvantaged groups. This half-day, in-person tutorial introduces a proposed design-and-audit framework that treats a user simulator as a verifiable engineering artefact composed of seven auditable components - structured Persona, task-aware Contract, matched human-vs-agent Execution, auditable Trace, persona-aligned Verification, structured Feedback, and a Refinement loop that updates personas and contracts. Through two hands-on mini-labs on recommendation-list evaluation and search-query formulation, participants will inspect simulator behaviour end-to-end, distinguish diagnostic discrepancy analysis from statistical validation, and apply checks for fidelity, credibility, and demographic bias. The tutorial targets information retrieval and recommender systems researchers and practitioners interested in user behaviour simulation and responsible AI.

</details>


### [105] [Private Information Retrieval for Large-Scale DNA-Based Data Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14557)
*Gökberk Erdoğan,Daniella Bar-Lev,Rawad Bitar,Antonia Wachter-Zeh,Zohar Yakhini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在合成DNA数据存储中实现私有信息检索（PIR）的两种方法，分析了生化查询机制带来的独特挑战，并展示了信息论隐私权衡在DNA存储系统中的体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将传统数字数据库的PIR技术扩展到基于DNA的存储系统面临生化查询机制及其复杂性的独特挑战，需要探索适应DNA存储的隐私保护检索方案。

Method: 提出两种适配双服务器PIR协议的方法，分别权衡隐私、效率和可行性，以应对DNA存储的生化特性。

Result: 两种方法展示了信息论隐私权衡在DNA存储系统中的具体表现形式，证明了PIR在DNA存储中的可行性。

Conclusion: DNA存储中的PIR存在独特的隐私-效率权衡，两种提出的方法为这一新兴领域提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate Private Information Retrieval (PIR) in the context of synthetic DNA-based data storage. While PIR is a well-studied primitive for digital databases, extending it to DNA-based databases presents unique challenges arising from biochemical query mechanisms and their complexity. We propose two approaches for adapting two-server PIR protocols to DNA-based storage, balancing privacy, efficiency, and feasibility. These approaches illustrate how information-theoretic privacy trade-offs manifest in DNA-based storage systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Do programming languages still matter to your AI coding agent teammate? Evidence at scale from chess engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13763)
*Mathieu Acher,Jean-Marc Jézéquel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探究前沿编码Agent的多语言编程能力，让Claude Code和Codex在17种编程语言（从主流到生僻语言如LaTeX和Brainfuck）中自主构建国际象棋引擎。结果显示Agent在所有目标语言中均能生成完整可运行的引擎，但语言选择显著影响性能（仅主流编译语言可达高Elo等级分）、开发成本（语言越生僻成本越高）和功能特性分布。Agent可自主验证但自我评估存在偏差，部分引擎会调用现成库"作弊"。结论：编程语言不再是AI能否构建系统的瓶颈，而是关乎性能、成本、构建内容及所需人类监督程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答两个实证问题：1) AI编码Agent能否在任何目标编程语言中编程，包括那些没有可比开源先例的语言？2) 语言选择是否仍会影响最终产物，以及影响体现在哪些维度上？

Method: 以国际象棋引擎为多组件系统的案例研究，选取17种从主流到生僻的编程语言，使用两个前沿Agent（Claude Code和Codex）在无象棋知识和实现指导的干预政策下构建引擎，结合特征分析、独立Elo评估、会话轨迹记录及代码与对话记录的定性分析。

Result: 所有目标语言均产生至少一个功能丰富的可用引擎，部分语言（如LaTeX）此前无同等规模的开源实现。但只有主流编译语言能达到强棋力（高Elo等级分）；语言越生僻，开发成本和工程投入增长越陡峭；不同语言族系间功能选择存在差异。Agent可自主验证，但自我棋力评估存在偏差，且有引擎通过调用现成象棋库"作弊"。

Conclusion: 前沿编码Agent具有真正的多语言编程能力，语言选择不再是能否构建工作系统的决定因素，而是关乎性能表现、开发成本、构建内容及所需人类监督验证的程度。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents now promise end-to-end authorship of complete software systems. Two empirical questions follow: can AI coding-agent teammates program in any target language, including ones with no comparable prior open-source artefact? If so, does language choice still shape the artefact, and along which dimensions? We study both through a polyglot case study built around chess engines: non-trivial multi-component systems that admit a hierarchy of language-agnostic oracles, from exact move-generation correctness to a strength scale (Elo), observable from Rust to Brainfuck. We prompted two frontier agents (Claude Code and Codex) at the capability level, without chess knowledge or implementation guidance, under a documented intervention and stopping policy. The agents produced 34 chess engines spanning 17 primary programming languages, from mainstream to specialised, domain-specific, legacy, and esoteric targets. We combine per-engine feature analysis, independent Elo assessment, and session trajectories with qualitative analysis of code and transcripts.
  Frontier coding agents are genuinely polyglot: every language we tried produced at least one feature-rich working engine, several with no prior open-source counterpart of comparable scope (e.g., LaTeX), and the code is synthesised from scratch rather than copied. Yet language choice still matters: strong playing strength is only reachable in mainstream compiled languages, cost and engineering effort grow sharply as the language becomes more exotic, and feature choices shift across language families. Agents validate their own work unprompted, but their strength self-estimates are biased and a few engines cheated by calling a chess library. Programming language is no longer about whether AI teammates can build a working system, but about performance, cost, what gets built, and how much human supervision validation still needs.

</details>


### [107] [A Benchmark and Framework for Evaluating Next Action Predictions in Spreadsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13802)
*Tejas Agrawal,Vu Le,Sumit Gulwani,Gust Verbruggen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对电子表格中预测性代码补全的基准测试，通过手动策划52个动作序列和使用在线评估方法，解决了缺乏编辑历史和动作空间复杂性的挑战，并分析了多种预测模型在不同属性上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预测性代码补全在编程中大幅提升了开发效率，但在更为普遍的电子表格场景中，类似的自动补全功能几乎不存在。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: (1) 手动策划52个序列共12K个动作，通过参数化启发式和LLM精炼从公开语料库重建电子表格；(2) 提出在线评估方法：在每个用户动作后预测后续动作，接受或拒绝预测，更新后续动作，重复直到目标表格达成。使用多种基线预测器（零样本LLM、微调SLM、经典模型）进行分析。

Result: 分析了保存动作和误报的属性、效率、用户画像影响、触发因素影响、上下文影响等多个维度。

Conclusion: 该基准为电子表格环境中的动作预测提供了系统化的评估框架，揭示了不同预测方法在电子表格场景下的特性与挑战。

Abstract: Predictive code completion greatly accelerates how quickly developers work. In spreadsheets, despite being much more common, such auto-completion features are virtually non-existent. To address this gap, we introduce a benchmark for systems that observe a sequence of user actions in a spreadsheet and predict future actions. Two challenges are (1) the absence of edit histories in public spreadsheet corpora and (2) the complex space of spreadsheet actions (spatial, temporal, composite). To address (1), we manually curate 52 sequences of 12K actions that recreate spreadsheets from public corpora, seeded by parametrized heuristics and LLM refinement. To address (2), we propose an online evaluation that expects a prediction after each user action, accepts or rejects that prediction, updates the future actions upon acceptance, and repeats this until the target spreadsheet is obtained. We use multiple baseline predictors (including zero-shot LLMs, fine-tuned SLMs, and classical models) and analyze different properties that our benchmark teaches us, including but not limited to: properties of saved actions and false positives, efficiency, effect of user profiles, effect of triggers, and effect of context.

</details>


### [108] [An Empirical Study of Gemini 3 for Detecting Natural Language Test Smells in Manual Test Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13804)
*Keila Lucas,Rohit Gheyi,Márcio Ribeiro,Fabio Palomba,Luana Martins,Elvys Soares*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究使用大语言模型（GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW）通过基于提示的完整测试用例分析策略，检测自然语言手工测试用例中的测试坏味。在100个Ubuntu测试用例上的实验表明，该方法优于之前的小语言模型（GEMMA-3-4B、LLAMA-3.2-3B、PHI-4-14B），能生成可操作的解释，并发现测试坏味平均每步接近一个，凸显了对手工测试工件进行自动化质量支持的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工测试用例中存在测试坏味（如歧义、冗余、缺失检查等），影响测试的可靠性、可维护性和可复现性。现有检测方法依赖人工规则，难以泛化和扩展。本文旨在探索使用大语言模型以完整测试用例分析的方式检测手工测试用例中的测试坏味，克服传统方法的局限性。

Method: 采用GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW大语言模型，通过提示（prompt-based）策略对完整测试用例进行整体分析，而非逐步骤孤立分析，从而捕捉测试步骤间的依赖关系。在100个Ubuntu手工测试用例上评估，覆盖7种测试坏味类型，并与GEMMA-3-4B、LLAMA-3.2-3B、PHI-4-14B三个小语言模型进行性能对比。

Result: GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW在检测性能上优于所有对比的小语言模型，并且能生成可操作的解释来帮助实践者修改测试用例。此外，研究发现测试坏味在实际中非常普遍，平均每个测试步骤检测到接近一个坏味。

Conclusion: 大语言模型通过完整测试用例分析策略能有效检测手工测试用例中的测试坏味，优于小语言模型，并提供有价值的可解释性。测试坏味的普遍性表明，需要可扩展的自动化质量支持工具来改善手工测试工件。

Abstract: Manual testing, in which testers follow natural language instructions to validate system behavior, remains essential for uncovering issues that are difficult to capture with automation. However, manual test cases often contain test smells, quality issues such as ambiguity, redundancy, or missing checks that reduce reliability, maintainability, and reproducibility. Existing detection approaches largely depend on manually engineered rules and thus struggle to generalize and scale across heterogeneous test suites. In our previous work, we assessed the feasibility of using Small Language Models (SLMs) for test smell detection by evaluating GEMMA-3-4B, LLAMA-3.2-3B, and PHI-4-14B on test steps from 143 real-world Ubuntu test cases, covering seven smell types. PHI-4-14B achieved the best performance. In this article, we investigate whether a contemporary Large Language Model (GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW) available at the time of the study can identify test smells in natural language manual test cases using a prompt-based, whole-test-case analysis strategy. Unlike approaches that analyze individual test steps in isolation, our approach evaluates complete test cases, enabling the model to consider relationships and dependencies among test steps. We evaluate the approach on 100 Ubuntu test cases covering seven test smell types and compare its performance against previously evaluated SLMs, including GEMMA-3-4B, LLAMA-3.2-3B, and PHI-4-14B. Our results show that GEMINI-3-PRO-PREVIEW outperforms the SLMs, while producing actionable explanations that can help practitioners revise manual test cases for greater clarity and consistency. We also find that test smells are pervasive in practice, with nearly one detected test smell per step on average, highlighting the need for scalable and automated quality support for manual testing artifacts.

</details>


### [109] [A Principled Framework for Safe Algorithm Updates in Automated Insulin Delivery Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13882)
*Thomas Screven,Ziqiang "Joe" Zhu,Deniz Cengiz,Rayhan A. Lal,Korey K. Hood,Samuel T. King*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于评估AID算法更新安全性的两阶段框架，包括错误分类（事实性、启发式、计算性）和临床等效性评估（基于误差网格分析）。通过在Trio系统从JavaScript移植到Swift的bug修复案例中验证，机械模拟和影子执行均表明新旧版本在临床指标上等效，超过99%的配对血糖值位于Parkes误差网格A/B区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共适应系统中，用户已围绕现有算法行为调整设置，算法bug修复可能反而破坏血糖控制。目前缺乏原则性框架来评估AID算法更新的安全性。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 将错误分为事实性(factual)、启发式(heuristic)、计算性(computational)三类，对应不同管理策略；2) 通过影子执行(736,480次调用)和临床等效性分析（配对血糖误差网格评估）验证。应用案例为Trio系统的oref算法从JavaScript到Swift的bug修复移植。

Result: 机械模拟中Swift与JavaScript版本TIR均为84.9%，GRI分别为23.5% vs 23.9%，>99%配对血糖在Parkes误差网格A/B区。影子执行中oref组件失配率极低(iob 0.43%, autosens 1.22%, determineBasal 0.07%, meal 0.01%)，仅有0.03%的iob调用产生临床意义差异。数据驱动回放模拟中>99%下游配对血糖在A/B区。

Conclusion: 该框架将bug修复原则与多方法临床评估相结合，可评估AID算法更新安全性，系统无关且适用于所有主流开源AID系统，案例研究表明需系统性修复事实性和计算性错误。

Abstract: Background: AID algorithms require ongoing software updates and bug fixes. In co-adapted systems, where users tune settings around existing algorithmic behavior, bug fixes can paradoxically disrupt glycemic control. No principled framework evaluates the safety of AID algorithm updates.
  Methods: Our two-part framework classifies bugs and evaluates the clinical equivalence of AID system software updates. Bugs are classified as factual, heuristic, or computational, each with distinct management strategies. Classifications were validated from porting Trio's oref algorithm from Javascript to a bug-fixed Swift implementation. We compared implementations using shadow execution on 736,480 invocations from eight Trio users. The second component assesses clinical equivalence with error analysis on paired glucose values, applied to both Trio implementations using mechanistic in silico and data-driven replay simulation.
  Results: In mechanistic in silico simulation, the Swift and Javascript implementations produced nearly identical Time in Range (84.9% vs. 84.9%) and Glycemia Risk Index (23.5% vs. 23.9%), with more than 99% of paired glucose in Parkes Error Grid Zones A and B, meeting our clinical equivalence threshold. Shadow execution showed low mismatch rates in oref components (iob 0.43%, autosens 1.22%, determineBasal 0.07%, meal 0.01%), with clinically meaningful differences in 0.03% of iob invocations. Data-driven replay simulations of bugs revealed more than 99% of downstream paired glucose in Parkes Error Grid Zones A and B, also meeting our clinical equivalence threshold.
  Conclusions: Our framework integrates bug-fixing principles with multi-method clinical evaluation to assess AID algorithm update safety. It is system-agnostic and applicable to all widely used OS-AID systems, with case studies highlighting the need for systematic remediation of factual and computational bugs.

</details>


### [110] [Bayesian-Calibrated Detection of Hallucinated Package Imports in AI-Assisted Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13918)
*Lom M. Hillah,Jean-Marc Richard,Ryan Hasnaoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯校准层，用于LLM代码中幻影包导入检测器。该层基于三类别认知分类学输出Beta后验概率，并利用PyPI元数据信号识别注册但可疑的包，支持下游CI门控的阈值决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有slopsquat检测器仅输出二元决策（标记/不标记），无法表达检测置信度，且仅依赖200/404注册表通道，难以识别注册但可疑的包——而这正是LLM后攻击的真实场景。

Method: 1) 3类别认知分类学将每个先验分类为经验校准、建设性论证或工程判断追溯；2) 基于贝叶斯推断输出Beta后验概率；3) 除注册表通道外，利用PyPI元数据信号（包年龄、发布次数、作者描述、摘要）检测注册但可疑的包。

Result: 在1,734个Python片段上评估（6个模型生成），校准层复现了严格注册表检测结果，并通过元数据通道引入了良好校准的额外标记。使用McNemar配对检验评估检测不对称性，使用ECE和Brier分数评估校准质量。

Conclusion: 所提校准层将二元检测扩展为概率输出，并扩展了检测维度，支持下游CI门控进行有原则的阈值决策，更适合实际LLM后攻击场景。

Abstract: We present a Bayesian calibration layer for slopsquat detectors -- those that flag hallucinated package imports in code produced by large language models (LLMs). Where existing pipelines emit binary decisions (flag / do-not-flag), our layer emits a Beta-posterior probability per detection, derived from a 3-category epistemic taxonomy that explicitly classifies each prior as empirically calibrated, constructively argued, or engineering-judgement-traced.
