Beyond the primary 200/404 registry channel, the calibrated layer exploits PyPI metadata signals -- package age, release count, author descriptor, summary -- to surface registered-but-suspicious packages that a binary registry detector misses, which is the realistic post-LLM-emission attacker regime. The resulting risk-aware primitive is directly consumable by downstream CI gates and supports principled threshold decisions across detection rules. We evaluate the calibration on a merged corpus of 1,734 Python snippets -- a stratified 189-prompt BigCodeBench slice plus a 100-prompt niche-library stress-test set, generated across a six-model panel spanning four cloud models (Claude-Sonnet-4.6, Mistral-Large, DeepSeek-v4-pro, DeepSeek-R1) and two local open-weight code models (Mistral Codestral, Meta CodeLlama). Against a re-implemented binary baseline inspired by Mahmud et al. -- which shares its registry oracle with our ground truth and therefore serves as a degenerate upper bound rather than a genuine competitor -- the calibrated layer reproduces the strict-registry detections and introduces well-calibrated additional flags on the metadata channel. We assess detector asymmetry with a McNemar paired test and calibration with both a flagged-subset Expected Calibration Error and a strictly proper full-corpus Brier score.

</details>


### [111] [LLM Agents Can See Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14061)
*Dongjian Ma,Silin Chen,Yufei Yang,Yulin Shi,Yanfu yan,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型在代码仓库级问题解决中，视觉表示作为文本补充模态的有效性，发现纯视觉方案效果不佳，但视觉辅助结合文本可在保持或提升准确率的同时降低高达26%的输入token消耗，混合文本-视觉设计是下一代编码智能体的实用方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体几乎完全以文本形式消费仓库代码，忽略了人类开发者利用文件夹层次结构和依赖关系等视觉结构在大型代码库中定位信息的方式。随着多模态大语言模型的发展，能否有效利用仓库的视觉表示是一个开放问题，亟需系统性实证研究。

Method: 对仓库级问题解决任务进行首次系统性实证研究，评估四种最新多模态模型，对比纯视觉、纯文本以及视觉作为补充模态的混合方案，分析不同模态设置下的准确率、token消耗以及视觉在不同阶段（如故障定位）和不同控制策略下的效用。

Result: 纯视觉方案因缺乏充分的符号细节且需反复视觉查询补偿，导致准确率下降和token成本增加；而将仓库结构的视觉图作为补充模态与标准文本接口结合，可帮助智能体更高效地理解结构，输入token消耗降低高达26%，同时问题解决准确率保持不变或有所提升。视觉表示在故障定位阶段以及智能体自主控制探索深度时最为有用。

Conclusion: 纯视觉方案不可行，但视觉作为文本的补充模态具有显著价值，混合文本-视觉设计是下一代编码智能体的实用方向。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models have demonstrated strong performance on software engineering tasks. Yet most agents consume repositories almost entirely as text, which differs from how human developers use visual structure such as folder hierarchies and dependency relationships to orient themselves in large codebases. With multimodal large language models (MLLMs), it is an open question whether agents can effectively benefit from visual representations of repositories. This paper presents the first systematic empirical study of visual repository representations for LLM-based agents on repository-level issue resolution. We evaluate four recent multimodal models. Our results show that a strictly vision-only setup degrades accuracy and increases token cost, because agents lack sufficient symbolic detail and compensate with repeated visual queries. In contrast, integrating visual graphs of repository structure as a supplementary modality alongside standard text interfaces helps agents understand structure more efficiently: input token consumption decreases by up to 26% while issue-resolution accuracy is maintained or improved. Visualization is most useful during fault localization and when the agent autonomously controls exploration depth. These findings point to a practical hybrid text-and-vision design for next-generation coding agents.

</details>


### [112] [FastContext: Training Efficient Repository Explorer for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14066)
*Shaoqiu Zhang,Maoquan Wang,Yuling Shi,Yuhang Wang,Xiaodong Gu,Yongqiang Yao,Rao Fu,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FastContext 是一个专用探索子代理，它将代码仓库探索与问题解决分离，通过并行工具调用和专注的上下文传递，在 SWE-bench 等基准上提升最高 5.5% 的解决率并降低最高 60% 的 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码代理在软件工程任务中表现出色，但仓库探索成为主要瓶颈：定位相关代码消耗大量 token 预算，并在代理上下文中混入无关片段。大多数代理中，同一模型既做探索又做求解，导致探索性读取和搜索遗留到求解器的历史中。

Method: 提出 FastContext，一个专用探索子代理，将仓库探索与求解分离。按需调用，通过并行工具调用返回简洁的文件路径和行范围作为聚焦上下文。由 4B-30B 参数的专用探索模型驱动，从强参考模型轨迹引导，并使用任务接地奖励进行细化，以支持广泛的首轮搜索、多轮证据收集和精确引文生成。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Multilingual、SWE-bench Pro 和 SWE-QA 上，将 FastContext 集成到 Mini-SWE-Agent 中，端到端解决率提升最高 5.5%，编码代理 token 消耗降低最高 60%，且开销微小。

Conclusion: 仓库探索可以与求解分离，并由专用模型有效处理。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) coding agents have achieved strong results on software engineering tasks, yet repository exploration remains a major bottleneck: locating relevant code consumes substantial token budget and pollutes the agent's context with irrelevant snippets. In most agents, the same model explores the repository and solves the task, leaving exploratory reads and searches in the solver's history. We present FastContext, a dedicated exploration subagent that separates repository exploration from solving. Invoked on demand, FastContext issues parallel tool calls and returns concise file paths and line ranges as focused context. FastContext is powered by specialized exploration models spanning 4B--30B parameters. We bootstrap them from strong reference-model trajectories and refine them with task-grounded rewards for broad first-turn search, multi-turn evidence gathering, and precise citation generation. Across SWE-bench Multilingual, SWE-bench Pro, and SWE-QA, integrating FastContext into Mini-SWE-Agent improves end-to-end resolution rates up to 5.5\% while reducing coding-agent token consumption up to 60\%, with marginal overhead. These results show that repository exploration can be separated from solving and handled effectively by specialized models. Code and data: https://github.com/microsoft/fastcontext

</details>


### [113] [Simulating Students' Java Programming Errors with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14113)
*Ali Keramati,Jie Cao,Iman Mohammadi,Mark Warschauer,Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用大语言模型（LLM）模拟学生编程逻辑错误的可行性。基于CodeWorkout数据集的74,000+份Java学生提交，评估了五种LLM在三种提示策略下的表现，发现Claude Sonnet 4在错误多样性与学生对齐性上表现最佳。盲审实验（N=401）证实合成错误与真实错误在功能上不可区分，但高难度问题会引发更多样化却更不像学生的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程教育中获取学生代表性错误集既慢且成本高，因为真实提交需要大量课堂部署后才能积累。本文探索LLM能否作为可扩展的代理来模拟学生逻辑错误，以加速错误数据的生成。

Method: 使用CodeWorkout数据集（37个问题，74,000+份学生Java提交），评估五种LLM在三种提示策略（IO、CoT、Self-Refine）下的表现。从多样性（错误模式范围）和对齐性（与真实学生错误的匹配度）两个维度衡量，并进行了盲审专家标注研究（N=401）对比合成错误与真实错误。

Result: 所有模型都能生成多样化的错误，但与学生提交的对齐程度存在差异。Claude Sonnet 4在平衡性上表现最佳。盲审表明合成错误与真实错误在功能上不可区分。高难度问题会引发更多样化但更像学生的错误。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效模拟学生编程错误，但在使用中需要在多样性与对齐性之间进行权衡。研究结果为将合成错误集成到可教学代理、智能辅导系统和大规模学习分析中提供了设计参考。

Abstract: Understanding student errors in the programming is a cornerstone of programming education, yet obtaining a representative set of student errors for any newly designed task remains slow and costly, since authentic submissions only accumulate after extensive classroom deployment. This paper explores whether large language models (LLMs) can serve as scalable proxies for students by simulating realistic logical errors in code submissions. Using the CodeWorkout dataset of 74,000+ unique student Java submissions across 37 problems, we evaluate five LLMs under three mainstream prompting strategies: Input-Output (IO), Chain-of-Thought (CoT), and iterative Self-Refine. We assess performance along two key dimensions: diversity (the range of distinct error patterns) and alignment (alignment with authentic student mistakes), and examine how these vary by struggling level of programming tasks. Our quantitative findings reveal that while all models generate diverse errors, their alignment to human submissions diverges: Claude Sonnet 4 achieves the most balanced performance. In addition, we conducted a blinded expert annotation study (N = 401) comparing synthetic and authentic errors. This qualitative analysis confirms that the generated errors are functionally indistinguishable from authentic student errors. Moreover, higher-struggling-level problems elicit more diverse but less student-like errors. These results highlight trade-offs in using LLMs to simulate human learners and suggest design considerations for integrating synthetic errors into teachable agents, intelligent tutoring systems, and large-scale learning analytics.

</details>


### [114] [Investigating Metamorphic Fuzz Oracle Enhancement via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14164)
*Ruixiang Qian,Ding Yang,Zengxu Chen,Yuxuan Gao,Chunrong Fang,Chao Zhang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于蜕变关系的模糊测试预言增强方法(MFOE)，并利用LLM框架MetaFOE自动生成和集成蜕变预言，在OSS-Fuzz驱动上实现平均18.7%的边覆盖提升和1,528个独特崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试驱动通常仅依赖基于崩溃的预言，忽视了库功能测试，限制了漏洞检测能力。构建和集成蜕变预言需要大量领域知识，自动化实现MFOE极具挑战性。

Method: 提出MetaFOE框架，利用LLM自动从蜕变关系(MR)中生成蜕变预言，并将其集成到现有模糊驱动中。在OSS-Fuzz驱动上使用三种现代LLM和五种提示策略进行实验评估。

Result: 生成了3,475个MR，其中77.3%可应用；实现了12,351个元驱动，其中6,228个有效。三小时模糊测试后，有效元驱动使边覆盖平均提升18.7%，触发1,528个独特崩溃。

Conclusion: 验证了蜕变预言增强在灰盒模糊测试中的有效性，以及使用LLM自动化MFOE的可行性，为推进灰盒模糊测试提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Fuzz drivers are essential components of greybox fuzzing, as they encapsulate target interfaces, define test spaces, and largely determine fuzzing effectiveness. Existing fuzz drivers typically rely on crash-based oracles for security testing, overlooking library functionality and limiting bug detection capability.
  In this paper, we present the first study on metamorphic-based fuzz oracle enhancement (MFOE), which augments existing fuzz drivers with metamorphic-based oracles derived from metamorphic relations (MRs). Since constructing and integrating such oracles requires substantial domain knowledge, automating MFOE is challenging. To address this challenge, we propose MetaFOE, an LLM-based framework that automatically generates and integrates metamorphic-based oracles.
  We evaluate MetaFOE on OSS-Fuzz drivers using three modern LLMs and five prompt strategies. MetaFOE generates 3,475 MRs, of which 77.3% are applicable, and implements 12,351 meta drivers, with 6,228 being valid. After three hours of fuzzing, the valid meta drivers improve edge coverage by an average of 18.7% and trigger 1,528 unique crashes. Our results demonstrate both the effectiveness of metamorphic-based oracle enhancement and the feasibility of using LLMs to automate MFOE, providing valuable insights for advancing greybox fuzzing.

</details>


### [115] [Evaluating LLMs for Obfuscation Detection and Classification in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14233)
*Luca Ferrari,Marco Alecci,Jordan Samhi,Tegawende' F. Bissyande',Jacques Klein,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在不依赖手工规则、预定义签名或专门训练的情况下，通过语义推理检测Android应用中代码混淆的能力，并与现有基于SAST的方法进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android应用开发者越来越多地使用代码混淆技术来阻碍逆向工程和保护知识产权，但混淆也降低了静态分析和漏洞检测工具的有效性。现有检测方法多依赖手工启发式规则或工程化特征，难以泛化到不断演化的混淆策略。

Method: 在包含多技术混淆的受控基准测试集和从Google Play收集的真实世界Android应用数据集上，评估多种开源和专有LLM在不同提示设计、模型选择和决策阈值下的混淆检测能力，并与现有SAST方法进行对比。

Result: LLM能够通过语义推理识别混淆代码，无需手工规则或专门训练；提示设计、模型选择和决策阈值对检测性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: LLM在Android安全分析中的混淆检测具有潜力，但仍存在局限性，需进一步研究以应对不断演化的混淆技术。

Abstract: Android applications (apps) developers increasingly rely on code obfuscation techniques to hinder reverse engineering and protect intellectual property. However, obfuscation also reduces the effectiveness of static analysis and vulnerability detection tools, creating challenges for Android security analysis. Existing approaches for detecting obfuscation in Android apps predominantly rely on handcrafted heuristics, engineered features, or task-specific learning pipelines, which may struggle to generalize across evolving obfuscation strategies.
  This paper presents a large-scale empirical study investigating the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to detect obfuscation in Android apps through semantic reasoning. Our study evaluates whether off-the-shelf LLMs can identify obfuscated code without relying on handcrafted rules, predefined signatures, or dedicated model training. The empirical evaluation is conducted on both a controlled benchmark containing an app obfuscated with multiple techniques and a real-world dataset of Android apps collected from Google Play. The study further examines the impact of prompt design, model selection, and decision thresholds across several open-weight and proprietary LLMs. Finally, the analysis compares LLM-based reasoning with existing SAST-based obfuscation-detection approaches and discusses the broader implications and limitations of applying LLMs to Android security analysis.

</details>


### [116] [I'm Sorry Driver, I'm Afraid I Can't Do That: Appraising the Safety of LLMs within Automotive Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14327)
*Shaun Feakins,Ibrahim Habli,Kim Littler,Robert Palin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了当前AI开发框架中，将大语言模型集成到汽车控制任务时的安全性保障问题，指出这些框架在实时安全关键场景中存在概念性和具体性挑战，并通过案例研究和安全论证揭示了现有解决方案的局限性，最后提出了面向未来的LLM相关危险事件的保障机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在汽车领域的集成正在加速，但现有框架在实时安全关键场景中的有效性不足，存在上游通用模型在下游车辆架构中的双重挑战，亟需从安全保证角度进行系统性评估。

Method: 通过概念性挑战分析（上游-下游双重挑战）和具体性挑战分析（ISO21448中的工程约束与ISO/PAS8800中的LLM对齐问题），并结合Talk2Drive开源仓库的实验案例研究，构建安全论证以阐明现有解决方案的局限性。

Result: 现有框架面临双重保障挑战：上游通用模型需在下游特定车辆架构中部署；存在ISO21448中的延迟等工程约束与ISO/PAS8800中LLM专属的对齐问题。案例研究证实了这些局限性。

Conclusion: 尽管LLM在汽车场景中的技术探索和运营化正在进行，但现有方案存在显著局限，需提出面向未来的LLM相关危险事件保障机制。

Abstract: This paper appraises recent frameworks within AI development to integrate LLMs into control tasks in automotive contexts from the perspective of safety assurance. This work has built upon the rapid integration of LLMs across automotive settings. However, we find that at present, these frameworks face significant challenges, limiting their efficacy in real-time safety-critical contexts. Firstly, we consider conceptual challenges, including the fact that deployers are faced with a dual challenge, wherein they must assure a model which has been developed upstream, i.e. as general-purpose tools by the large AI labs, in a downstream context, i.e. into specific vehicle architectures. Secondly, we consider concrete challenges from across existing standards. We show that there are currently both fundamental engineering constraints covered in ISO21448, such as latency, and novel LLM-specific issues, such as alignment-related issues covered in ISO/PAS8800. We ground both examples in a concrete introductory, experimental case study exploring an existing open-source repository, Talk2Drive. We present a safety argument in order to make explicit the limitations of existing solutions. Nonetheless, given that the use of LLMs in automotive contexts is being explored at a technical level and operationalised, we propose potential assurance mechanisms for LLM-related hazardous events going forward.

</details>


### [117] [No Accidental Software Agent First Canonical Code for Human Code Entropy Reduction and 30 to 500 times Lower Frontier Model Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14357)
*Jepson Taylor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体优先的规范代码"概念，通过行为等价商化消除偶然复杂性，将代码重写为带有证明义务的规范行为档案，目标是将已验证正确变更的全成本降低约100倍。在Qwen2.5-Coder-14B上的初步QLoRA实验表明规范轨迹可学习，但未验证行为保持和规模经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码模型在人类代码库中花费大量容量学习偶然熵（如框架变动、命名漂移、生成源歧义等），而非真正的程序行为。这些有价值信号与大量无关噪声纠缠在一起，导致模型容量浪费和验证成本高昂。

Method: 提出智能体优先的规范代码，一种带有证明的基底：通过行为等价商化将等效编码折叠为受治理的代表，附带显式证据和证明义务。具体包括规范行为档案、类型化变更代数、证明通道、约束编辑文法、语义补丁单元、运行时负记忆和携带证明的变更对象。

Result: 初步QLoRA实验在Qwen2.5-Coder-14B上使用64,088条规范轨迹进行训练，结果表明轨迹可学习且能抑制测试中的禁用语言标记。但未建立行为保持性、规模经济性或已验证变更成本。报告的成本缩减区间是假设而非实测结果。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出一个可证伪的研究纲领，围绕最小功能描述长度和已验证变更成本两个核心指标。理论极限是"无事故视界"——可移除偶然性持续减少直至剩余新颖性、证据、治理、风险和未来可选性主导。对于支持的常规产品软件，给出了约100倍全面成本降低的可防御规划目标，但非对所有软件的保证。

Abstract: Frontier coding models may spend substantial capacity learning not only program behavior, but also accidental entropy in human repositories. Such repositories contain valuable signals: tests, incidents, migrations, edge cases, product judgment, and operational history. These signals are entangled with framework churn, naming drift, generated-source ambiguity, dependency rituals, CI dialects, weak proof routes, and human-oriented review customs. We propose agent-first canonical code, a proof-carrying substrate that rewrites routine product software into canonical behavior profiles, typed change algebra, proof lanes, constrained edit grammars, semantic patch cells, runtime negative memory, and proof-carrying change objects.
  The core hypothesis is that quotienting software by behavior equivalence under a declared oracle can collapse equivalent encodings into governed representatives with explicit evidence and proof obligations. The endpoint is amortized cost per verified correct change, including source, context, reasoning, tools, verification, security, provenance, review, failed loops, defects, and foundry cost under a common oracle. Reported reduction bands are hypotheses, not measured frontier results. The proposed limit is a No-Accident Horizon: removable accident decreases until residual novelty, evidence, governance, risk, and future optionality dominate. For supported routine-product distributions, this gives a defensible planning target near 100-fold all-in cost reduction, not a guarantee for all software. Preliminary QLoRA experiments on Qwen2.5-Coder-14B show that 64,088 canonical trajectories are learnable and suppress tested forbidden-language markers, but do not establish behavior preservation, scaling economics, or verified-change cost. The contribution is a falsifiable program centered on minimum functional description length and verified-change cost.

</details>


### [118] [tap: A File-Based Protocol for Heterogeneous LLM Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14445)
*Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 tap，一种基于文件的协作协议，允许 Claude (Anthropic) 和 Codex (OpenAI) 等不同供应商的 LLM 代理在无需共享内存或相同运行环境的情况下，通过文件优先设计（文件通信 Tier1 + 实时通知 Tier2）和 git worktree 隔离在同一代码库上协作。在 27 天的自我应用中，异质模型对的缺陷检出率为 69.8%，高于同质模型对的 53.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多代理软件系统假设共享运行环境、中央会话服务器或相同 API 家族，导致不同供应商的 LLM 代理无法在各自执行环境中直接交换消息并协同开发。

Method: tap 采用文件优先设计：将 Markdown 文件（含元数据）作为原始消息持久化（Tier1 文件通信），结合 Claude 和 Codex 的实时通知路径（Tier2 实时通信），并通过独立 git worktree 隔离工作；即使实时通知失败或接收方重启，消息文件仍可被重新检查。

Result: 在 27 天 37 次自我应用中，收集 209 个 PR 和 717 个操作工件；分析 375 个审查工件显示，异质模型对（Claude+Codex）缺陷/变更请求检出率为 69.8%，同质模型对（相同模型）为 53.1%。

Conclusion: tap 在实际生产仓库中成功运行，结合文件消息持久化与实时通知的混合模式有效；异质模型与执行环境的组合能拓宽审查视角，提升缺陷检出能力。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent software development systems have proposed many forms of agent collaboration, including role-based collaboration and automated code review. However, many systems assume a common runtime, a central conversation server, or the same API family. Under these assumptions, LLM agents from different vendors cannot easily exchange messages directly from their own execution environments while dividing development and review work on a shared codebase. This paper presents tap, a file-based collaboration protocol that allows Claude (Anthropic) and Codex (OpenAI) to collaborate on one codebase without shared memory or an identical runtime. The core of tap is a file-first design that preserves markdown files with metadata as original messages, combines a file inspection path (file communication, Tier 1) with real-time notification paths for Claude and Codex (real-time communication, Tier 2), and isolates work through separate git worktrees. Even if real-time notification fails or a receiver restarts, the message file remains available and the same content can be inspected again. In a 27-day, 37-generation self-applied operation where tap was used to develop and review itself, we collected 209 tap-related pull requests and 717 operational artifacts. An analysis of 375 review artifacts showed that the share of reviews recording at least one defect or requested change was 69.8% for heterogeneous model pairs and 53.1% for homogeneous model pairs. These results show that tap, which combines file-based message preservation with real-time notification, operates in a real production repository, and that combining heterogeneous models and execution environments can broaden review perspectives. tap is distributed as the open-source npm package @hua-labs/tap (v0.5.2).

</details>


### [119] [Security Threats and Their Impact on Blockchain Interoperability: Identification and Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14554)
*Shawn M. Reynolds,Hassan Reza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统性地识别和分类了跨链互操作中的安全威胁（分为五类：核心区块链攻击、网络攻击、互操作特定攻击、社会工程和代码漏洞），并针对每类威胁提出防御策略，构建了分类体系以支持安全跨链方案的设计与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链互操作性在实现跨异构网络通信与资产交换的同时，缺乏全面的安全机制，攻击者已导致数亿美元的资产损失，亟需系统性的威胁识别与分类。

Method: 通过系统化方法，将安全威胁分为五类（核心区块链攻击、网络攻击、互操作特定攻击、社会工程、代码漏洞），对每类威胁分析攻击面并提出相应的防御策略。

Result: 构建了一个结构化的安全威胁分类体系，涵盖五类威胁及对应的防御策略。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为设计和评估安全的区块链互操作解决方案提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: Blockchain interoperability enables independent blockchain systems to communicate and exchange assets across heterogeneous networks. However, the lack of comprehensive security mechanisms remains a critical weakness -- one that attackers have already exploited to cause hundreds of millions of dollars in asset losses. This paper presents a systematic identification and classification of security threats facing interoperable blockchain systems, along with corresponding countermeasures for each. We organize threats into five categories: (1) core blockchain attacks, (2) network attacks, (3) interoperability-specific attacks, (4) social engineering, and (5) code vulnerabilities, with particular attention to smart contract weaknesses. For each identified threat, we analyze its attack surface and propose effective defensive strategies. The resulting taxonomy provides a structured foundation for designing and evaluating secure blockchain interoperability solutions.

</details>


### [120] [When Errors Become Narratives: A Longitudinal Taxonomy of Silent Failures in a Production LLM Agent Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14589)
*Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对长期运行的个人助理LLM智能体运行时系统，进行了为期八周的纵向研究，记录了22起事故（至少28次无声故障）。提出五类机制导向的分类法，其中D类（链式幻觉与捏造）是LLM系统独有的最危险类型——系统不仅不报错，反而用流畅可信的叙述欺骗用户，作者称之为"fail-plausible"（失败得合理）。关键发现：70%的无声故障由人类用户视角发现，而非测试或审计；事后审计能阻止87%回归，但无法事前预防；故障延迟（13小时至60天）与失败机制相关，而非代码复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统越来越多地作为长期运行的自主运行时存在（调度任务、调用工具、维护记忆、向人类推送结果），但这类系统中无声故障（silent failures）的发生模式、分类和防御机制尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 对一个自2026年3月起持续生产的个人助理智能体运行时进行八周纵向研究，该系统约40个定时任务、8个LLM提供商、一个工具治理代理、一个知识库记忆平面，受4286个单元测试和827个治理检查保护。记录了22起事故并完成完整根因分析，基于故障机制推导出五类分类法。

Result: 提出了五类无声故障分类：(A) 环境与平台异常、(B) 设计假设不匹配、(C) 错误吞没与稀释、(D) 链式幻觉与捏造（LLM特有）、(E) 操作遗漏与取证盲点。发现约70%的无声故障由人类用户视角捕获；审计能阻止87%回归但无法事前预防；故障延迟与失败机制而非代码复杂度相关。

Conclusion: 提出了针对智能体系统的防御框架和设计原则，目标是让故障变得"响亮、可归因且无聊"（loud, attributable, and boring）。所有事后分析和工件均已公开。

Abstract: LLM agent systems increasingly run as long-lived autonomous runtimes: scheduling jobs, calling tools, maintaining memory, and pushing results to humans. We present a longitudinal study of silent failures in one such system: a personal-assistant agent runtime in continuous production since March 2026, with roughly 40 scheduled jobs, 8 LLM providers, a tool-governance proxy, and a knowledge-base memory plane, defended by 4,286 unit tests and 827 governance checks. Over eight weeks we documented 22 incidents with full root-cause postmortems, in which one meta-pattern -- a failure whose error signal never reaches a human in actionable form -- manifested at least 28 times. We derive a five-class, mechanism-oriented taxonomy: (A) environment and platform quirks, (B) design-assumption mismatches, (C) error swallowing and dilution, (D) chained hallucination and fabrication, (E) operational omission and forensic blind spots. Class D is unique to LLM systems and the most dangerous: the system does not merely fail to report an error -- the LLM transforms it into fluent, plausible narrative delivered to the user. We term this fail-plausible: gray failure's differential observability escalated -- the observer is not just blind, it is convincingly lied to by the failure itself. Three findings: about 70% of silent failures were caught by human user-view observation, not tests or audits; a retrospective audit of 15 incidents found 0% ex-ante prevention but 87% regression blocking -- audits are regression engines, not prediction engines; incident latency (13 hours to 60 days) tracks failure mechanism, not code complexity -- the longest-lived failures lived in the seams between components, where no test runs. We describe the resulting defense framework and distill design principles for agent systems whose failures are loud, attributable, and boring. All postmortems and artifacts are public.

</details>


### [121] [Regulating the Machine Contributor: Governance and Policy Alignment in Open Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14594)
*Jassem Manita,Aziz Amari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI辅助软件开发对开源项目贡献流程的冲击，通过对六个开源组织的政策比较，提出了六维分类法、政策成熟度评分，并将现有AI治理框架与开源贡献政策之间的缺口进行了映射，最终勾勒了分层协调框架的雏形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主与半自主AI贡献者破坏了传统开源贡献流程中关于法律责任人、来源证明和审稿互动的假设，2025-2026年间发生的AI驱动事件、AI生成的噪声提交及平台级封禁表明这一差距已产生实际运维后果，亟需系统性的政策分析。

Method: 采用最相似系统设计（Most-Similar Systems Design），结合基于指标的编码和过程追踪方法，对SymPy、LLVM、matplotlib、OpenInfra、Apache软件基金会和Linux基金会六个组织的贡献政策进行比较分析。

Result: 生成了六维分类法（披露、责任、人类监督、许可、执行、维护者工作量）、序数性政策成熟度评分，以及AI代理事件在各政策未治理维度上的映射关系。

Conclusion: 现有开源贡献政策与AI治理框架（EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF及UC Berkeley Agentic AI Profile、ISO/IEC 42001和23894）之间存在重叠缺口，需构建分层协调框架并通过实证评估进行校准。

Abstract: AI-assisted software development has moved from line-level autocomplete to agents that can plan changes, edit files, and submit pull requests with limited human supervision. Open-source software, however, evolves through a process designed for humans: contributor agreements, codes of conduct, and review norms all assume a legally accountable person who can attest to provenance and answer reviewer questions. Autonomous and semi-autonomous AI contributors strain those assumptions, and the 2025-2026 record of agent-driven incidents, AI-generated nuisance volume, and platform-level shutdowns shows that the gap is operationally consequential. Several open-source organisations have responded with contribution policies, but the result is fragmented, and its alignment with emerging AI governance frameworks (EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF with the UC Berkeley Agentic AI Profile, ISO/IEC 42001 and 23894) is unmapped at the contribution level. We compare policies across six organisations (SymPy, LLVM, matplotlib, OpenInfra, the Apache Software Foundation, and the Linux Foundation) using Most-Similar Systems Design with indicator-based coding and process tracing for SymPy and LLVM. From this we derive a six-dimensional taxonomy (disclosure, responsibility, human oversight, licensing, enforcement, maintainer workload), an ordinal Policy Maturity Score, and a mapping of documented agent incidents onto the dimensions each policy fails to govern. Aligning the dimensions with the regulatory frameworks above identifies overlapping gaps neither side currently closes, and we close by sketching the shape of a harmonised tiered framework and the empirical evaluation needed to calibrate it.

</details>


### [122] [Empowering Student Debugging in Parallel Programming with Execution Traces and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14607)
*God'salvation F. Oguibe,Vinodh Kumaran Jayakumar,Tongping Liu,Andrew Lan,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款名为ParaView的教育工具，通过透明执行记录与可视化帮助学生理解和调试C/C++并发程序中的死锁、竞态条件等问题。实验表明，使用ParaView的学生在调试和实现任务上表现显著提升；同时探索了LLM辅助分析并发错误，但修复正确性在复杂场景下仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发编程是计算机科学教育中的核心难点，学生难以掌握由不确定性和竞态条件、死锁等并发错误带来的复杂性，亟需有效的教学工具辅助理解与调试。

Method: 开发ParaView工具，提供透明的执行记录与可视化功能，使并行执行过程可观测；并探索利用大语言模型（LLM）分析并发错误及建议修复方案。通过17名学生参与的调试与实现任务实验评估工具效果。

Result: 与以往课程迭代相比，学生在调试和实现任务上的成功率显著提升；学生调查显示多数参与者认为ParaView有帮助。LLM在识别错误和解释执行轨迹方面高效，但修复复杂同步模式时的正确性参差不齐。

Conclusion: 基于记录-可视化的工具（如ParaView）辅以AI（如LLM）可以有效改进并发编程的教学与学习效果。

Abstract: Concurrent programming is a core component of Computer Science curricula, yet remains notoriously difficult for students to master due to its inherent complexity and the nondeterministic nature of concurrency bugs such as deadlocks and race conditions. In this work, we present ParaView, an educational tool designed to help students understand, debug, and correct concurrency issues in parallel programs written in C/C++. ParaView provides transparent execution recording and visualization to make parallel execution observable and comprehensible. We evaluated ParaView through a series of debugging and implementation tasks, with 17 students participating. Results showed a significant improvement in debugging and implementation successes compared to previous course iterations. A student survey confirmed that most participants found ParaView helpful. To further support learning outside the classroom, we explored using Large Language Models (LLMs) to analyze concurrency bugs and suggest fixes. While LLMs were highly effective in identifying bugs and explaining execution traces, the correctness of their bug fixes varied, especially for more complex synchronization patterns. Our findings suggest that recording-visualization tools like ParaView, complemented by artificial intelligence (AI), can improve teaching and learning of concurrent programming.

</details>


### [123] [GitHub Template Repositories: Served Domains, Maintenance, and Practitioner Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14616)
*Leuson Da Silva,Altaf Allah Abbassi,Imen Trabelsi,Paulo Borba,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对GitHub模板仓库进行了大规模实证研究，覆盖五种主流编程语言。通过LLM-as-a-judge策略分类模板领域，分析仓库特征与质量问题的关联，并定性推导模板设计实践指南。结果表明Web开发是主要领域，维护和质量因语言而异，高质量模板常采用成熟软件工程实践并配有完善文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub自2019年引入模板仓库功能用于代码复用，但对其支持的领域、维护特性及有效设计实践知之甚少，因此开展该大规模实证研究以填补这一空白。

Method: 大规模实证研究：1) 挖掘并分类五种主流编程语言的模板仓库，采用LLM-as-a-judge策略分析服务领域；2) 通过统计分析探索仓库特征与活动、质量问题（代码坏味、漏洞、安全热点）的关联；3) 定性分析代表性模板子集，推导设计与管理指南及常见陷阱。

Result: Web开发是跨生态系统的主导领域；维护和质量问题因编程语言而异；高质量模板倾向于采用成熟软件工程实践，并提供全面的文档和清晰的使用指南。

Conclusion: 研究结果为 practitioners 设计和采用高质量模板仓库提供了实证洞察和可操作指南。

Abstract: Over time, GitHub has introduced different strategies for sharing reusable code artifacts. In addition to fork-based reuse, template repositories provide a distinct feature for generating new projects from scaffolding. Although this feature has been available since 2019, little is known about the domains it supports, its maintenance characteristics, or the practices that guide practitioners for effective template design. To address this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of GitHub template repositories across the five most used programming languages. First, we mine and categorize templates to analyze the domains they serve, exploring the LLM-as-a-judge strategy. Next, we explore the reliability of templates by evaluating the associations between repository characteristics and activity, and quality-related issues (e.g., code smells, vulnerabilities, and security hotspots) through statistical analysis. Finally, we qualitatively analyze a representative subset of templates to derive practical guidelines and recurring pitfalls for template design and management. Our results show that Web Development is the predominant domain across ecosystems, while maintenance and quality issues vary by programming language. We further find that high-quality templates tend to adopt established software engineering practices, while providing comprehensive documentation and clear guidance for use. Overall, our findings offer empirical insights and actionable guidance to support practitioners in designing and adopting high-quality template repositories.

</details>


### [124] [Teaching Machine Learning to Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14618)
*Nafiseh Kahani,Jason Jaskolka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过编制AI/ML相关主题清单、映射现有SE课程覆盖情况、调查教师优先级，为在本科SE课程中系统整合AI/ML内容提供了基于证据的指导建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML组件越来越多地嵌入软件产品中，但本科SE课程很少系统性地教授如何构建、测试、部署和维护基于AI/ML的软件系统，存在课程与行业需求之间的脱节。

Method: 1) 编制并定义与AI/ML软件相关的SE实践主题清单；2) 将这些主题映射到代表性SE课程的核心必修课，识别覆盖缺口；3) 调查SE教师对主题重要性和整合约束的评估；4) 基于主题定义、课程覆盖和教师优先级的交叉分析，推导出指导建议。

Result: 生成了结构化的AI/ML相关主题清单，识别了现有SE课程中的覆盖缺口，获取了教师对主题优先级和整合可行性的反馈，并形成了将高优先级主题嵌入现有SE课程的具体指导建议。

Conclusion: 提出了基于证据的指导方针，建议在现有SE课程中何处以及如何嵌入高优先级的AI/ML相关主题，从而为SE课程改革提供了可操作的方向。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) components are increasingly embedded in software products, yet undergraduate software engineering (SE) curricula rarely provide systematic preparation for building, testing, deploying, and maintaining AI/ML-based software systems. This paper aims to provide evidence-based guidance for integrating AI/MLrelevant content into core SE education. We compile and define a structured inventory of topics relevant to SE practice in AI/MLbased software, then map these topics against required courses in a set of representative SE curricula to identify coverage gaps. To assess educational priorities and feasibility, we survey SE instructors on topic importance and integration constraints. Based on the crosswalk between topic definitions, curriculum coverage, and instructor prioritization, we derive a guideline that recommends where and how high-priority topics can be embedded within existing SE courses.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-Based Transformer Method for Solving the Open Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13682)
*Faezeh Ardali,Mwembezi A. Nyelele,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer编码器-解码器架构的开放车间调度问题（OSSP）求解策略，仅使用加工时间矩阵作为输入，在Taillard小规模基准实例（4×4至10×10）上训练，无需重新训练即可泛化至大规模随机实例（40×40至100×100），其最大完工时间与最优已知值差距在15-30%以内，在大规模问题上平均gap为12.89-15.12%，优于SPT和LPT，与EST竞争相当，展现了良好的跨规模泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSSP在工业和服务场景中广泛存在，但随着作业和机器数量增加，精确方法难以求解，传统调度规则和元启发式方法在大规模问题上需要大量调参才能保持解质量，因此亟需一种无需特征工程、可跨规模泛化的学习型调度方法。

Method: 采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构，利用多头自注意力机制；仅以加工时间矩阵为输入；在Taillard小规模基准实例（4×4、5×5、7×7、10×10）上训练；训练后直接应用于大规模问题（40×40至100×100）进行零样本迁移测试。

Result: 在小规模基准上，生成可行调度且最大完工时间在最优已知值的15-30%以内；在大规模随机实例上，平均gap为12.89-15.12%，优于SPT和LPT，与EST差距微小，展现了良好的竞争力和泛化性。

Conclusion: 在小规模OSSP实例上训练的Transformer策略能够有效泛化到大规模问题，提供了一种轻特征、基于学习的调度规则替代方案，为学习型方法在组合优化问题中的应用提供了有力证据。

Abstract: The open shop scheduling problem (OSSP) arises in many industrial and service settings but remains computationally challenging as the number of jobs and machines increases. While exact methods quickly become intractable, classical dispatching rules and metaheuristics may require substantial tuning to maintain solution quality at large scales. This study develops a Transformer-based scheduling policy for OSSP using an encoder-decoder architecture with multi-head attention. The model is trained on Taillard benchmark instances (4x4, 5x5, 7x7, and 10x10) using only the processing-time matrix as input and produces feasible schedules with makespans typically within 15-30% of best-known values. To evaluate scalability, the trained policy is applied without retraining to randomly generated instances from 40x40 to 100x100 and compared against classical dispatching heuristics, including SPT, LPT, MWKR, and EST. Across these large instances, the Transformer achieved average gaps of 12.89-15.12% relative to a standard lower bound. Compared with EST, the Transformer remained competitive, typically within a modest margin, while substantially outperforming SPT and LPT. These results indicate that a Transformer policy trained on small OSSP instances can generalize to substantially larger problems and provide a feature-light, learning-based alternative to classical dispatching rules.

</details>


### [126] [UP-NRPA: User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation for Planning with Large Language Models in Goal-oriented Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13683)
*Hui Wang,Fafa Zhang,Meng Liu,Xiangyu Chen,Chaoxu Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于用户画像的嵌套推出策略自适应（UP-NRPA）在线框架，利用大语言模型实现对话策略的动态定制，无需离线强化学习，在协作与非协作对话基准测试中取得显著效果，谈判任务中销售列表比率提升56.41%，多个对话任务实现100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话策略规划方法难以动态适应多样化的用户特征，传统方法依赖模型训练和离线强化学习策略模型，无法针对不同用户群体进行灵活调整。

Method: 提出UP-NRPA在线框架，利用大语言模型，通过自适应机制结合实时用户反馈以及从当前用户画像中映射的个性、偏好和目标，实现对话策略的动态定制，无需离线强化学习。

Result: 在协作与非协作对话基准测试中，UP-NRPA在多个对话任务上实现100%成功率；在谈判任务中，销售列表比率（SL）提升56.41%。

Conclusion: UP-NRPA无需训练机制即可适应多样化的用户需求，使对话系统能够动态适配用户特征。

Abstract: To address the challenge that current dialogue policy planning methods struggle to dynamically adapt to diverse user characteristics, this paper proposes a User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (UP-NRPA) online framework with Large Language Models. In contrast to conventional approaches dependent on model training and require offline reinforcement learning policy models for user groups, UP-NRPA enables dynamic customization of dialogue strategies through an adaptive mechanism. This is achieved by leveraging real-time user feedback alongside personality, preferences, and objectives mapped from the current user portrait, thereby adapting to user characteristics without offline reinforcement learning. In collaborative and non-collaborative dialogue benchmarks, UP-NRPA demonstrated considerable benefits, achieving an impressive 100% success rate in multiple dialogue tasks. Particularly in negotiation tasks, the sale-to-list ratio (SL) increased by 56.41%. This demonstrates that UP-NRPA can adapt to diverse user needs without requiring a training mechanism, enabling the dialogue system to adapt to user characteristics.

</details>


### [127] [History of the Muddy Children Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13703)
*Hans van Ditmarsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文追溯了“泥孩子谜题”在过去两个世纪中通过逻辑与文学出版物的起源，探讨其对认知逻辑发展的启发，并提出了一个涉及自指的新型帽子谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泥孩子谜题是认知逻辑发展的关键启发，但它的首次提出者尚不清楚。本文旨在澄清其起源，并扩展该谜题的类型。

Method: 通过梳理过去两个世纪的逻辑与文学出版物，追溯泥孩子谜题的历史起源；同时设计一个涉及自指的新型帽子谜题作为变体。

Result: 发现了泥孩子谜题的多重历史渊源，并成功构建了一个包含自指机制的新型帽子谜题变体。

Conclusion: 泥孩子谜题的起源比通常认为的更早且更复杂，自指型帽子谜题是该经典谜题的一个有意义的新拓展。

Abstract: The Muddy Children Puzzle is a puzzle about knowledge and ignorance that has been inspiring for the development of epistemic logic. Who came up with it first? This is unclear. We trace the origin of the Muddy Children Puzzle through logical and literary publications over the past two centuries. The puzzle inspired a numerous variations such as involving numbers or coloured hats. We also present a novel hats puzzle involving self-reference.

</details>


### [128] [Orchestra-o1: Omnimodal Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13707)
*Fan Zhang,Vireo Zhang,Shengju Qian,Haoxuan Li,Hao Wu,Jinyang Wu,Donghao Zhou,Zhihong Zhu,Zheng Lian,Xin Wang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Orchestra-o1，一种全模态智能体编排框架，通过统一编排机制实现模态感知的任务分解、在线子智能体专业化和并行子任务执行，在 OmniGAIA 基准上超越第二名 10.3% 准确率，并引入 DA-GRPO 强化学习方法训练出 SOTA 的开源全模态智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体编排框架局限于少数模态，难以泛化到异构模态共存和交互的复杂全模态场景，亟需一种支持多种模态（文本、图像、音频、视频）统一理解和协调的编排框架。

Method: 提出 Orchestra-o1 全模态智能体编排框架，包含统一编排机制，支持模态感知的任务分解、在线子智能体专业化和并行子任务执行；并引入决策对齐的群体相对策略优化（DA-GRPO）用于高效训练。

Result: 在 OmniGAIA 基准上超越第二名 10.3% 准确率；Orchestra-o1-8B 达到开源全模态智能体 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: Orchestra-o1 有效解决了全模态场景下的智能体编排问题，其可扩展设计能够处理涉及异构信息源的复杂真实世界任务。

Abstract: The recent success of agent swarms has shifted the paradigm of large language model (LLM)-based agents from single-agent workflows to multi-agent systems, highlighting the importance of agent orchestration for task decomposition and collaboration. However, existing orchestration frameworks are limited to a narrow set of modalities and struggle to generalize to more complex settings where heterogeneous modalities coexist and interact. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced in omnimodal scenarios, where tasks require the unified understanding and coordination of diverse inputs such as text, image, audio, and video. In this work, we propose Orchestra-o1, an omnimodal agent orchestration framework designed to support efficient agent collaboration across multiple modalities. Orchestra-o1 introduces a unified orchestration mechanism that enables modality-aware task decomposition, online sub-agent specialization, and parallel sub-task execution. This scalable design allows agent systems to effectively tackle complex real-world tasks involving heterogeneous information sources, surpassing the second-best approach by 10.3% accuracy on the OmniGAIA benchmark. Furthermore, we introduce decision-aligned group relative policy optimization (DA-GRPO), an efficient agentic reinforcement learning approach for training Orchestra-o1-8B, which also achieves state-of-the-art performance against all existing open-source omnimodal agents.

</details>


### [129] [Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution Makes Better Deep Researcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13710)
*Hongming Piao,Chi Liu,Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Derek Li,Ying Wei,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出混合开放式三元进化（HOTE）框架，通过混合模式强化学习实现提议者、求解者和评判者的协同进化，在长形式深度研究基准上，8B模型超越静态开源8-32B模型及现有深度研究方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究受限于静态参数化能力，而智能体进化仅在可验证的标准答案任务上有效，两者之间存在鸿沟，无法直接应用于开放式研究任务。

Method: 提出HOTE框架，采用混合模式强化学习，基于网络规模知识促进提议者（proposer）、求解者（solver）和评判者（judge）三个模块的协同进化。

Result: 在三个长形式深度研究基准上，8B模型超越最强的静态开源8-32B模型以及现有深度研究训练方法，且时间开销更少。

Conclusion: HOTE中三个模块的进化缺一不可，该框架有效弥合了深度研究与智能体进化之间的差距，推动智能体在开放式任务中自主进化。

Abstract: Deep research and agent evolution serve as de-facto tasks for AI agents in real-world applications toward artificial general intelligence. The former enables autonomous retrieval and integration of information in open-ended environments to tackle open-ended research tasks, yet it is constrained by the static parametric deep research capabilities of agent systems. The latter allows agents to autonomously interact with the environment to gain experiences that evolve model capabilities. However, its effectiveness has been widely validated only on verifiable tasks with standard answers, leaving a gap with open-ended research tasks. To bridge these two critical tasks, we propose the Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution (HOTE) framework, which leverages hybrid-mode reinforcement learning to facilitate the collaborative evolution of a proposer, solver and judge based on web-scale knowledge, moving toward autonomous evolving agents in open-ended tasks and environments. Extensive experiments on three long-form deep research benchmarks demonstrate that the 8B model trained via HOTE surpasses the strongest static open 8-32B models as well as those trained by state-of-the-art deep research training methods with less time overhead, and further verify that the evolution of all three modules in HOTE is indispensable.

</details>


### [130] [WorkBench Revisited: Workplace Agents Two Years On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13715)
*Olly Styles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkBench基准测试显示，前沿AI代理从2024年3月到2026年6月取得了巨大进步：GPT-4完成43%任务（26%有害行为）→Claude Opus 4.8完成89%任务（2.5%有害行为）。能力与安全性协同提升而非权衡，但前沿模型仍存在导致不可逆伤害的基本错误。开源模型大幅降低了高性能门槛，而前沿成本保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估前沿AI代理在能力与安全性方面的进展，量化从2024年到2026年间智能体性能的提升幅度，并揭示能力与安全性之间的关系。

Method: 在WorkBench基准测试上对比2024年3月的最佳代理GPT-4与2026年6月的最佳代理Claude Opus 4.8，统计任务完成率与无意有害行为发生率，同时分析错误类别变化和模型成本趋势。

Result: 最佳代理任务完成率从43%提升至89%，无意有害行为从26%降至2.5%；能力与安全性正相关而非权衡；部分错误类别已完全消除，但基本错误仍存在并可能导致不可逆伤害；开源模型使高性能变得低成本可及。

Conclusion: 前沿AI代理在两年间取得了显著进步，能力与安全性协同提升，但基本错误仍未完全解决，需持续关注。开源模型的发展打破了专有模型的高性能垄断。

Abstract: The best agent on WorkBench in March 2024, GPT-4, completed 43% of tasks and took an unintended harmful action, such as emailing the wrong person, on 26% of them. We re-visit the benchmark in June 2026 and find that the best agent to date, Claude Opus 4.8, completes 89% and takes an unintended harmful action on 2.5%. Aside from this considerable progress in frontier agent performance, three things stand out. First, capability and safety go together on WorkBench rather than trade off, so the models that finish the most tasks also do the least unintended damage. Second, while several classes of error have been totally eliminated, frontier models still make some basic mistakes that occasionally result in irreversible harm, such as sending an email to the wrong person. Third, the rise of open-weight models has drastically lowered costs for a performance level that was previously only accessible to proprietary models, while frontier costs have stayed relatively stable. We release an updated version of the benchmark with data and code quality improvements, new model scores, and analysis of agent progress on WorkBench since 2024.

</details>


### [131] [Refusal Beyond a Single Direction: A Preliminary Comparison of Diff-in-Means and INLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13720)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种干预方法（DiM和INLP）在安全微调对话模型上抑制拒绝行为的效果，发现INLP反事实翻转可媲美DiM方向消融，而零空间投影效果较弱；将INLP限制为前导方向可在保持抑制效果的同时实现可调能力，且两种INLP干预在激活空间中产生不同的几何分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究能否通过INLP方法（零空间投影和反事实翻转）匹配已有的DiM方法（激活添加和方向消融）在控制安全微调模型的拒绝行为上的效果，以及INLP更丰富的参数化是否能够提供更可调的干预。

Method: 在五个开源对话模型上，比较DiM两种干预（激活添加、方向消融）与INLP两种干预（零空间投影、反事实翻转）的效果；通过限制INLP子空间的前导方向来评估可调性；分析不同干预在激活空间中的几何分布差异。

Result: INLP反事实翻转在抑制拒绝行为上与DiM方向消融效果相当，但零空间投影效果明显较弱；将INLP限制到提取子空间的前导方向，可以在接近基线困惑度的条件下保留大部分抑制效果，从而获得可调节的能力；两种INLP干预在几何上产生定性不同的激活分布——零空间投影将激活值坍缩到有害与无害聚类的中间，而反事实翻转将其移动到相反的聚类中。

Conclusion: INLP反事实翻转是一种有效的拒绝抑制方法，可媲美DiM方向消融；模型编码"概念的缺失"与"概念的对立面"在几何上存在本质差异，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Arditi et al. (2024) has shown that refusal in safety fine-tuned chat models is mediated by a single linear direction in the residual stream, recoverable by a difference-in-means (DiM) of harmful and harmless activations. We compare DiM-based interventions (activation addition and directional ablation) with two interventions derived from Iterative Nullspace Projection (INLP) -- nullspace projection and counterfactual flipping -- on five open-weight chat models, asking whether INLP can match DiM at steering refusal and whether its richer parameterisation yields more tweakable interventions. INLP counterfactual flipping is competitive with DiM directional ablation on refusal suppression, while nullspace projection is consistently weaker. Restricting INLP to the leading directions of the extracted subspace preserves most of the suppression effect at near-baseline perplexity, giving a tunable capability. Geometrically, the two INLP interventions land in qualitatively different regions of activation space: nullspace projection collapses transformed activations \emph{between} the harmful and harmless clusters, while counterfactual flipping moves them into the opposite cluster, suggesting that the model encodes the absence of a concept differently from its opposite -- an intriguing distinction that warrants further investigation in future work.

</details>


### [132] [YeasierAgent: Agentic Social Sandbox as a Canvas for Intent-Driven Creation of Platform-Agnostic Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13722)
*Jory He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YeasierAgent提出了一种基于共生智能体、叙事世界和场景感知交互的应用构建范式，将软件从设备耦合模型重新定义为用户、智能体和世界之间的协作空间，通过平台无关的交互单元（智能体、场景、对话）实现跨平台智能体原生应用的快速构建，并在一个体验沙盒中统一了智能体的情感陪伴与实用工具执行属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统的设备耦合软件模型，解决现有应用开发中固定图形布局的局限性，以及智能体在情感陪伴和工具执行属性上的分离问题，推动从孤立工具型聊天机器人向社会化嵌入式计算环境的转变。

Method: 提出系统架构，利用平台无关的交互单元（智能体、场景、对话）替代固定图形布局；集成自动化生成、用户创建世界和空间多智能体协作；通过共生智能体原生应用范式的形式化定义，构建统一的体验沙盒。

Result: 实现了跨平台智能体原生应用的快速构建，并在同一体验沙盒中统一了智能体的情感陪伴与实用工具执行属性，形式化了共生智能体原生应用这一新类别。

Conclusion: YeasierAgent展示了从孤立工具型聊天机器人向 cohesive（内聚的）、社会化嵌入式计算环境的转变，为智能体原生应用的构建提供了新的范式。

Abstract: This paper introduces YeasierAgent, an application-building paradigm based on symbiotic agents, narrative worlds, and scene-aware interaction. It challenges the conventional device-coupled model of software by redefining applications as collaborative spaces among users, agents, and worlds. We present a system architecture that achieves two primary contributions: (1) enabling the rapid, cross-platform construction of agent-native applications by utilizing platform-agnostic interactive units (agents, scenes, dialogue) rather than fixed graphical layouts; and (2) unifying the emotional companionship and practical tool execution attributes of intelligent agents within a single experiential sandbox. By integrating automated generation, user-created worlds, and spatial multi-agent collaboration, YeasierAgent formalizes the category of Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications, demonstrating a shift from isolated, tool-specific chatbots toward cohesive, socially embedded computational environments.

</details>


### [133] [TwinBI: An Agentic Digital Twin for Efficient Augmented Interactions with Business Intelligence Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13731)
*Jisoo Jang Wen-Syan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinBI是一个结合LLM代理与可执行仪表板状态的数字孪生框架，通过共享分析状态统一对话交互、仪表板操作和语义理解，在A/B基准测试中将精确匹配准确率从43.3%提升至63.3%，超时率从40%降至10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业智能中仪表板交互与LLM辅助在多步分析时常出现状态不一致，用户在直接操作和自然语言查询间切换时难以保持统一的过滤、层级、指标和图表上下文。

Method: 提出TwinBI框架，包含基于LLM的代理系统与可执行BI仪表板状态的双生结构，通过统一交互日志重建共享分析状态，整合对话交互、仪表板操作、语义锚定和溯源追踪，并暴露模式视图、SQL、日志及/insights命令等分析工件。

Result: 在A/B基准测试中，TwinBI将精确匹配准确率从43.3%提升至63.3%，部分信度准确率从48.3%提升至70.8%，超时率从40%降至10%；可用性研究中参与者受益于集成仪表板与聊天工作流。

Conclusion: TwinBI通过将可见的仪表板状态转化为更丰富的可操作上下文，同时提升了代理级分析可靠性和面向用户的分析支持。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) increasingly combines dashboard interaction with LLM-based assistance, but these two modes often fall out of sync during multi-step analysis. As users switch between direct dashboard manipulation and natural-language queries, it becomes difficult to preserve a consistent analytical state across filters, hierarchies, metrics, and chart context. We present TwinBI, an agentic digital-twin framework that couples an LLM-based agent system with an executable BI dashboard state. TwinBI unifies conversational interaction, dashboard manipulation, semantic grounding, and provenance tracking through a shared analytical state reconstructed from a unified interaction log. It also exposes artifacts such as schema views, SQL, logs, and an /insights command for state-grounded analytical summaries. We evaluate TwinBI in two complementary ways. In a controlled A/B benchmark with the same backbone agent, TwinBI improves exact-match accuracy from 43.3% to 63.3%, partial-credit accuracy from 48.3% to 70.8%, and substantially reduces timeout rate from 40.0% to 10.0% relative to Dashboard alone. In a usability study, participants benefited from the integrated dashboard-and-chat workflow, with high task accuracy, moderate workload, and favorable ratings for state-aware interaction mechanisms. These results suggest that TwinBI improves both agent-level analytical reliability and user-facing analytical support by turning visible dashboard state into richer actionable context. Our dataset and source code are available at: https://github.com/simonjisu/TwinBI

</details>


### [134] [When Sample Selection Bias Precipitates Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13732)
*Xinbao Qiao,Xianglong Du,Wei Liu,Jingqi Zhang,Peihua Mai,Meng Zhang,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 低资源验证场景下，基于本地碎片化参考分布的数据选择会加剧而非缓解递归训练中的模型坍塌，通过Wasserstein代理参考分布可缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归合成数据训练虽能缓解数据稀缺，但存在模型坍塌风险；数据选择被认为是一种补救措施，但其可靠性高度依赖验证器使用的参考分布，而在低资源数据孤岛场景下，本地参考分布不完整，选择本身会产生偏差。

Method: 理论证明孤岛式选择加速坍塌并导致幂律多样性衰减；提出不使用原始数据共享的情况下，从多个数据孤岛构建Wasserstein代理参考分布作为初步缓解方案。

Result: 实验表明局部参考选择在偏态分布上失效，而协作式代理参考分布能缓解多样性退化。

Conclusion: 递归合成数据管道在真实数据覆盖零碎或稀缺时需要特别谨慎；孤岛式数据选择反而会加速模型坍塌，多源协作的代理参考分布是有效的缓解方向。

Abstract: The proliferation of recursive training on synthetic data can alleviate data scarcity but risks model collapse, where repeated training erodes distributional tails and homogenizes outputs. Data selection is widely viewed as a remedy, yet its reliability depends critically on the reference distribution used by the verifier. We show that in low-resource verification regimes, where each verifier observes only a small, fragmented, and biased slice of the target manifold, selection itself becomes biased. This situation naturally arises in low-resource data silos such as healthcare consortia or proprietary financial institutions, where raw data cannot be pooled and local references are inherently incomplete. As a result, selection preferentially retains samples aligned with the local manifold while pruning globally relevant tail modes, turning from a safeguard against collapse into a mechanism that precipitates it. We theoretically prove that such siloed selection accelerates collapse and induces power-law diversity decay. As an initial mitigation, we construct Wasserstein proxy references from multiple silos without sharing raw data. Empirical results confirm that local-reference selection fails on skewed distributions, whereas collaborative proxy references mitigate diversity degradation, suggesting that recursive synthetic-data pipelines require particular caution when real-data coverage is fragmented or scarce.

</details>


### [135] [AI Receptivity or AI Adoption Breadth? A Tool-Specific Reanalysis of the Lower-Literacy/Higher-Usage Link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13734)
*Hristo Inouzhe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究重新分析了Tully等人(2025)研究3的数据，发现AI素养与AI使用之间的负相关关系因工具类型而异：AI素养对文本类AI使用预测不显著，但对非文本类AI使用有显著负向预测作用，且这种关系主要体现在采用与否而非使用强度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并重新检验Tully等人(2025)提出的"较低的AI素养预测更高的AI接受度"这一笼统结论，揭示其背后的异质性。

Method: 使用Tully等人研究3的公开数据，采用OLS、二元Logit、有序Logit和多项Logit等多种规格进行回归分析，并对工具类型（文本AI与非文本AI）进行分层分析。

Result: AI素养与文本AI使用无显著关联（有序Logit β=-0.090, p=.387），但与非文本AI使用显著负相关（β=-0.377, p<.001）。非文本效果主要是采用/未采用的二元模式（经人口统计调整后的优势比OR=0.68），而非使用强度的证据。

Conclusion: 不支持"低AI素养预测更高AI总体接受度"的简单论断，而是表明该模式仅适用于普及率较低的非文本AI工具。

Abstract: Recent evidence reported by Tully, Longoni, and Appel (2025) suggests that lower artificial intelligence (AI) literacy predicts greater receptivity toward AI. We revisit this claim using the public data from Study 3 of that article, which measures past usage of five AI tool categories on a five-point frequency scale. We first reproduce the negative association between AI literacy and aggregate AI usage using OLS on participant-level averages, binary logit, ordered logit, and multinomial logit specifications. We then show that the aggregate relationship masks substantial heterogeneity by tool type. In our demographic-adjusted primary specification, AI literacy does not significantly predict text AI usage (ordered-logit $β$ = -0.090, p = .387), whereas it remains a strong predictor of non-text AI adoption ($β$ = -0.377, p < .001). The non-text effect is also robust under Tully et al.'s original Study 3 control specification ($β$ = -0.502, p < .001). Binary, ordered-logit, and multinomial specifications suggest that the non-text relationship is primarily an adoption/non-adoption pattern rather than evidence of intensive use: the demographic-adjusted odds ratio of ever having used a non-text AI tool is 0.68. Thus, in the study that measures self-reported past usage rather than stated preferences, the evidence does not support a simple claim that lower AI literacy predicts greater receptivity to AI in general. It points instead to a narrower pattern of broader adoption across lower-penetration, non-text AI tools.

</details>


### [136] [MA-ProofBench: A Two-Tiered Evaluation of LLMs for Theorem Proving in Mathematical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13782)
*Lushi Pu,Weiming Zhang,Xinheng Xie,Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Hongya Lyu,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Yudong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-ProofBench，据我们所知是首个专注于数学分析的形式化定理证明基准，包含200个覆盖6大核心主题和27个子类别的形式化定理，分为本科水平（Level I）和博士资格考试水平（Level II）。评估显示最强模型GPT-5.5在Level I上Pass@8仅16%，Level II仅5%，大多数模型在Level II上接近0%。失败模式主要为Mathlib幻觉和不完整证明，且自然语言与形式化推理之间存在明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明基准在数学覆盖范围和难度上存在局限，主要集中在代数、初等数论等易于形式化的领域，对需要更深层推理的数学分析等子领域覆盖不足，因此需要构建专门针对数学分析的形式化定理证明基准。

Method: 构建了包含200个形式化定理的基准，覆盖6大核心主题和27个子类别；通过人工主导、LLM辅助的形式化pipeline并经过独立专家审查来确保忠实于原始数学；将问题分为本科和博士资格考试两个难度级别；评估了多种通用推理模型和形式化定理证明器。

Result: 最好模型GPT-5.5在Level I上Pass@8仅16%，Level II仅5%；大多数模型在Level II上接近0%；失败模式主要是Mathlib幻觉和不完整证明；自然语言版本上模型表现远优于形式化版本，揭示了非形式化推理与形式化推理之间的明显差距。

Conclusion: MA-ProofBench可作为追踪高级领域形式化数学推理进展的可靠参考基准，当前LLMs在高级数学分析的形式化推理上仍然能力不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made notable progress in automated theorem proving, yet existing formal benchmarks remain limited in both mathematical coverage and difficulty. Most are concentrated in areas that are easier to formalize, such as algebra and elementary number theory, and provide limited coverage of subfields that require deeper reasoning, including mathematical analysis. To address this gap, we introduce MA-ProofBench, to the best of our knowledge, the first formal theorem-proving benchmark dedicated to Mathematical Analysis. The benchmark contains 200 formalized theorems covering 6 core topics and 27 subcategories, including measure and integration theory, complex analysis, and functional analysis. The problems are divided into two difficulty levels, an undergraduate level (Level I, 100 problems) and a Ph.D. qualifying level (Level II, 100 problems), to evaluate how well LLMs perform formal reasoning at different mathematical depths. Each problem is constructed through a human-led, LLM-assisted formalization pipeline followed by independent expert review, ensuring that the formal statements remain faithful to the original mathematics. We evaluate a range of recent general-purpose reasoning models and formal theorem provers on MA-ProofBench. However, most models perform poorly: even the best-performing model, GPT-5.5, achieves only 16% Pass@8 on Level I and 5% on Level II, while most models stay close to 0% on Level II. Further analysis identifies Mathlib hallucinations and incomplete proofs as the two dominant failure modes, while an evaluation on the natural-language version of the benchmark exposes a clear gap between informal and formal reasoning. MA-ProofBench is intended to serve as a reliable reference for tracking progress in formal mathematical reasoning in advanced domains.

</details>


### [137] [Poker Arena: Multi-Axis Profiling of Strategic Reasoning and Memory in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13815)
*Pratham Singla,Shivank Garg,Vihan Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Poker Arena平台，通过三层记忆架构和九轴认知剖面，揭示多维度评估下前沿LLM的策略推理能力结构，发现标量排行榜系统性地误判模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博弈基准将异质推理维度压缩为单一标量，未深入考察LLM在策略推理方面的能力结构。

Method: 构建Poker Arena无限注德州扑克锦标赛平台，包含三层记忆架构（局内、会话、跨会话）和九轴认知剖面对策略推理进行可解释维度分解，对七个前沿模型进行50轮×1000手牌的评估及记忆消融实验。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6赢得+$15,730筹码和14次第一名，但在均轴评分上仅排第五；持久记忆对部分模型有益而对其他模型有害。

Conclusion: 多轴评估揭示了标量排行榜系统性误判的能力结构，跨维度一致性比单一维度的峰值表现更重要。

Abstract: Strategic reasoning under uncertainty underpins consequential decisions in negotiation, finance, and policy, but prevailing game-play benchmarks collapse heterogeneous reasoning dimensions into a single scalar, leaving the capability structure of frontier LLMs unexamined. We introduce Poker Arena, a no-limit Texas Hold'em tournament platform that couples a three-layer memory architecture (within-hand, session, and cross-session) with a nine-axis cognitive profile decomposing strategic reasoning into interpretable dimensions such as bet-sizing calibration and positional awareness. We evaluate seven frontier models across 50 sessions of 1,000 hands and a controlled memory ablation; tournament chips and aggregate axis score order the field differently: Claude Opus 4.6 wins +$15,730 chips with 14 first-place finishes, yet ranks only fifth of seven on mean axis score, while persistent memory helps some models and hurts others. These findings show that multi-axis evaluation surfaces capability structure that scalar leaderboards systematically misrank, with cross-dimensional consistency outweighing peak performance on any single axis.

</details>


### [138] [Hyperdimensional computing for structured querying on tabular data embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13871)
*Sebastián Bugedo,Stijn Vansummeren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用超维计算（HDC）中的全息简化表示（HRR）模型进行表格行嵌入，通过代数性质推导可解释的相似度阈值，解决了现有嵌入方法无法进行零匹配检测的根本缺陷，在行检索任务中匹配或超越基线方法EmbDI，并实现完美的属性投影精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据嵌入方法（如EmbDI）无法提供可解释的相似度分数，导致无法设定有原则的检索阈值，特别是在零匹配检测场景中无法判断最近邻是真正匹配还是"最不相似"的无效结果，严重制约了实际部署。

Method: 采用超维计算（HDC）框架中的全息简化表示（HRR）模型进行表格行嵌入；利用HDC操作的代数性质，推导了等式和非等式检索谓词的闭式期望相似度值，这些值随维度增加收敛到可解释的数值，并据此识别合适的检索阈值。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明：HDC在所有配置下匹配或超越了EmbDI的行检索性能；更鲁棒地处理非等式谓词；在足够维度下实现完美的属性投影精度；通过有原则的阈值唯一实现了可靠的零匹配谓词检测。

Conclusion: HDC/HRR模型作为表格行嵌入框架，不仅保持了良好的检索性能，还通过可解释的相似度阈值解决了嵌入方法长期存在的零匹配检测难题，为数据集成管道提供了更可靠的部署方案。

Abstract: Tabular data embeddings have become a cornerstone of data profiling and data integration pipelines, enabling tasks such as entity annotation and resolution; schema matching; column type detection; and table search, among others. Existing approaches embed rows, columns, or entire tables into a vector space and rely on nearest-neighbor search to retrieve candidate matches. A fundamental limitation of current embedding methods is the lack of interpretable similarity scores: the concrete similarity value between a query and its nearest neighbour carries no intrinsic meaning, making it impossible to determine whether that neighbour is a true match or simply the least-dissimilar item in a corpus that contains no valid answer. This inability to set principled thresholds for retrieval undermines practical deployment, particularly for zero-match detection.
  We investigate the use of HyperDimensional Computing (HDC), specifically the Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) model, as a framework for tabular row embeddings when the retrieval task corresponds to answering structured select-project queries in vector space. Exploiting the algebraic properties of HDC operations, we derive closed-form expected similarity values for both equality and non-equality retrieval predicates, which converge to interpretable values as dimensionality increases, and use these to identify suitable retrieval thresholds. We evaluate HDC against EmbDI, a graph-based baseline, on two real-world datasets across varying table sizes and predicate lengths. Our results show that HDC matches or outperforms EmbDI for row retrieval across all configurations, handles non-equality predicates more robustly, and achieves perfect attribute projection accuracy at sufficient dimensionality -- while uniquely enabling reliable identification of zero-match predicates through its principled thresholds.

</details>


### [139] [Capability Minimization as a Safety Primitive: Risk-Aware Causal Gating for Least-Privilege LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13884)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知因果门控（RACG）框架，通过结合因果效应估计与校准风险控制，决定是否执行、延迟或弃权模型预测，在高风险场景下显著降低高成本错误，并优于基于置信度和选择性预测的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代决策系统依赖的学习组件可能输出自信但错误的预测，导致下游行动产生高昂代价。现有基于置信度的决策方法无法区分预测不确定性与因果风险，需要一种显式分离因果风险与预测不确定性的机制来提高决策安全性与透明度。

Method: RACG框架通过三个核心组件实现风险感知门控：(1) 因果效应估计，建模从候选行动到结果的因果路径；(2) 基于反事实风险的决策门控，替代原始预测置信度；(3) 分布无关的高风险行动概率边界，转化为满足用户指定安全约束的操作阈值；以及自适应门控策略，通过监控预测与实际结果的差异来应对分布偏移。

Result: 在模拟干预和真实决策基准上，RACG显著降低了高成本错误，同时保留了未门控策略的大部分效用，并在匹配弃权率下优于基于置信度和选择性预测的基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式分离因果风险与预测不确定性能够构建更安全、更透明的决策系统，为高风险场景下的可信自动化提供原则性机制。

Abstract: Modern decision systems increasingly rely on learned components whose outputs may be confident yet wrong, exposing downstream actions to costly errors. We introduce Risk-Aware Causal Gating (RACG), a framework that decides whether to act on, defer, or abstain from a model's prediction by combining causal effect estimation with calibrated risk control. RACG models the causal pathway from candidate actions to outcomes and gates each decision according to an estimated counterfactual risk rather than raw predictive confidence. To make gating reliable, we derive distribution-free bounds on the probability of acting under high-risk conditions and show how these bounds translate into operating thresholds that satisfy user-specified safety constraints. We further propose an adaptive gating policy that adjusts to distribution shift by monitoring discrepancies between predicted and realized outcomes, tightening the gate when causal assumptions appear violated. Across simulated interventions and real-world decision benchmarks, RACG reduces high-cost errors substantially while preserving most of the utility of an ungated policy, and it outperforms confidence-based and selective-prediction baselines at matched abstention rates. Our results indicate that explicitly separating causal risk from predictive uncertainty yields decision systems that are both safer and more transparent, offering a principled mechanism for trustworthy automation in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [140] [A Multi-Agent AI System for Automated High School Transcript Processing: Collaborative Document Analysis at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13916)
*Ben Torkian,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个多智能体AI系统，通过三个专门智能体（模式识别、语义分析、视觉智能）协同处理多样化高中成绩单，以GPA提取作为协调信号实现质量控制，在40份真实成绩单上达到96.7%准确率，每份处理时间45秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学招生办公室每年面临处理数百万份格式各异的高中成绩单的挑战，手动处理造成运营瓶颈，延迟录取决定并消耗大量资源。

Method: 构建多智能体架构，包含三个专门智能体（模式识别智能体负责格式特定解析、语义分析智能体负责自然语言理解、视觉智能智能体负责多模态文档分析），由编排智能体协调智能体通信和结果整合。创新点在于使用GPA提取作为协调信号进行基于智能体的质量控制。

Result: 在来自美国13个州的40份真实成绩单上评估，系统成功处理所有文档，与专家手动审核相比达到96.7%准确率，每份成绩单处理速度为45秒。

Conclusion: 多智能体协调可以解决复杂的文档处理挑战，为机构提供可扩展的协作式AI解决方案，在保持准确性的同时大幅减少处理时间。

Abstract: Each year, college admissions offices face an overwhelming challenge: processing millions of high school transcripts, each with unique formats, grading systems, and layouts. This manual process creates operational bottlenecks that delay admissions decisions and consume valuable resources. We present a transformative solution through a multi-agent AI system where specialized agents collaborate to automatically process diverse transcript formats through intelligent coordination and communication. Our multi-agent architecture consists of three specialized agents-a Pattern Recognition Agent for format-specific parsing, a Semantic Analysis Agent for natural language understanding, and a Vision Intelligence Agent for multimodal document analysis-coordinated by an Orchestration Agent that manages agent communication and result reconciliation. Our key innovation lies in agent-based quality control using GPA extraction as a coordination signal, ensuring reliable agent collaboration and preventing critical information loss. When evaluated on 40 real world transcripts from high schools across 13 U.S. states, our agent system successfully processed every document, achieving 96.7% accuracy compared to expert manual review while maintaining practical processing speeds of 45 seconds per transcript. This work demonstrates how multi-agent coordination can solve complex document processing challenges, offering institutions a scalable, collaborative AI solution that preserves accuracy while dramatically reducing processing time.

</details>


### [141] [Sorries Are Not the Hard Part: An Expert-Review Case Study of a Semi-Autonomous Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13925)
*Vasily Ilin,Brian Nugent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Grothendieck消没定理的半自动化形式化案例研究，指出大型语言模型在交互式定理证明中能填补证明空白，但编译通过的定理不等同于可复用的库贡献。经专家评审发现定义、通用性、文件组织和API存在严重问题，后续通过评审驱动的重构改进了可检查的局部问题，但在定义选择和API设计上仍然薄弱。作者主张自动形式化评估不应仅关注无报错编译，而应考察其能否通过专家评审。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化研究主要关注模型能否填补证明空白（即消除报错），但忽略了编译通过的证明在实际库贡献中的可复用性和质量。作者希望通过案例研究揭示这一差距，并推动评估标准从"无报错"转向"专家评审通过"。

Method: 选取Grothendieck消没定理作为典型案例，先用大语言模型半自动化完成形式化（编译无报错），然后邀请领域专家进行第一轮评审，识别定义、通用性、文件组织和API方面的严重问题；接着进行评审驱动的重构与压缩，再进行第二轮专家评审，对比前后结果。

Result: 模型在局部、机械可检查的反馈（如编译错误）上调整良好，但在需要全局设计的定义选择和API设计上表现薄弱；前后对比显示两者存在明显割裂。

Conclusion: 自动形式化的评估应当超越"无报错编译"这一标准，采用专家评审作为更严格的评价指标，因为可复用的库贡献需要正确的定义抽象和良好的API设计，而这些正是当前模型的主要短板。

Abstract: Large language models can often close proof gaps in interactive theorem provers, but a verified theorem is not the same thing as a reusable library contribution. We study this distinction through a detailed case study: a semi-autonomous formalization of Grothendieck's vanishing theorem. The initial version compiles with no sorries, but an expert review found serious problems in definitions, theorem generality, file organization, and the API. We then ran a review-driven refactor and compression process and obtained a second expert review. The before-and-after comparison shows a sharp split: agents adapted well to local, mechanically checkable feedback, but remained weak at choosing definitions and designing APIs. We argue that autoformalization should be evaluated not only by closed sorries, but by whether the resulting formalization survives expert review.

</details>


### [142] [Adversarial Concept Search: Predicting Compositional Errors From Feature Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13934)
*Jennifer Meng Lu,Ruochen Zhang,Isabelle Lee,David Alvarez-Melis,Ellie Pavlick,Naomi Saphra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析LLM的表征几何结构，预测模型在哪些概念组合上会失败。研究发现，当概念编码近似正交时模型能成功组合，而当编码接近产生干扰时则失败。该方法无需评估具体输入即可可靠预测不同组合任务中的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类无法直观判断哪些场景对LLM最具挑战性，现有方法依赖人工设计难题或大规模基准测试。本文旨在通过模型内部的表征几何结构主动预测模型的失败场景，而非被动测试。

Method: 利用LLM的表征几何（编码向量间的空间关系）来预测概念组合的成败：当两个概念的线性编码近似正交时模型能成功组合，编码接近（产生干扰）时模型失败。在玩具编程、多跳推理、多语言事实回忆等系统性组合任务上验证。

Result: 在多种需要系统组合的任务中，概念对编码近正交时可靠组合，编码接近导致干扰时模型失败。该方法无需评估具体输入即可预测失败模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用表征几何识别高风险样本、构建针对性压力测试以及为实际部署中的主动学习提供可扩展基础。

Abstract: Humans cannot always intuit what scenarios are most challenging to LLMs. Hoping to capture challenging edge cases, developers either design problems to be difficult for humans or curate extensive benchmarks. What if we could instead anticipate which scenarios a model will fail on? In this paper, we use an LLM's representational geometry to predict which concept combinations it will fail on. We attribute this compositional failure to interference between salient features. In tasks that require systematic composition - toy programmatic settings, multihop reasoning, multilingual factual recall - we find that when a pair of concepts is encoded near-orthogonally, the model reliably composes them. When their linear encodings are close, producing interference, the model fails to compose them. Our method reliably anticipates failure modes across different compositional tasks, without evaluating specific inputs. These results lay the groundwork to use representational geometry to identify high-risk examples, construct targeted stress tests, and provide a scalable foundation for active learning in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [143] [Minim: Privacy-Aware Minimal View for Agents via Trusted Local Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13949)
*Hexuan Yu,Chaoyu Zhang,Heng Jin,Shanghao Shi,Ning Zhang,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MINIM是一种在客户端运行的隐私感知UI状态最小化代理，通过Contextual Integrity框架学习每个UI元素的敏感度分数和任务必要性分数，实现三元披露策略（保留必要内容、抽象敏感属性、移除无关内容），在保持任务关键信息的同时显著减少敏感信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的自主智能体在复杂数字环境中依赖完整的UI状态观测来实现可靠的行动锚定，但将完整UI状态传输到远程推理服务器会泄露敏感但不必要的上下文信息（如验证码、私密通知、后台应用状态），存在隐私风险。

Method: 提出MINIM框架，在客户端侧部署可信本地代理进行隐私感知的UI状态最小化。基于Contextual Integrity理论，为每个UI元素学习双重分数：固有敏感度分数(s)和任务条件必要性分数(n)，并据此执行三元披露策略：保留必要元素、抽象敏感属性、移除任务无关内容。优化CI感知目标函数，对高风险内容上的必要性误差施加更强惩罚。

Result: 在WebArena派生的真实UI观测数据上实验表明，MINIM在保留任务关键语义上下文和交互能力的同时，显著减少了与任务无关的敏感信息泄露。

Conclusion: MINIM通过客户端侧本地代理和CI驱动的双重评分机制，能够在保障自主智能体任务执行能力的前提下有效降低隐私泄露风险，为LLM智能体的安全部署提供了一种实用的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM-powered autonomous agents increasingly rely on rich user interface (UI) state observations to achieve reliable action grounding in complex digital environments. However, many deployments transmit the full UI state to remote inference servers even when most elements are irrelevant to the current task, which can leak sensitive but unnecessary context such as authentication codes, private notifications, and background application states. We propose MINIM, a trusted local broker that performs privacy-aware minimization on the client side before any observation leaves the device. Grounded in Contextual Integrity (CI), MINIM learns a dual-score representation for each UI element by predicting an inherent sensitivity score (s) and a task-conditioned necessity score (n). These scores drive a ternary disclosure policy that keeps essential elements, abstracts sensitive attributes when needed, and removes task-irrelevant content. We optimize a CI-aware objective that penalizes necessity errors more strongly on high-risk content, enabling aggressive pruning while preserving task-critical information. Experiments on real-world UI observations derived from WebArena show that MINIM substantially reduces task-irrelevant sensitive leakage while preserving task-critical semantic context and the interactive affordances required for reliable agent actions.

</details>


### [144] [Formalizing Numerical Analysis: An Agent Pipeline and Quality Audit Beyond Kernel Acceptance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14000)
*Theodore Meek,Siyuan Ge,Di Qiu Xiang,Simon Chess,Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现有代码智能体形式化数学教材局限于mathlib已有分支且仅以编译通过为评价标准的问题，将智能体应用于形式化数值分析教材（ODE数值方法），并提出了一个三维评估框架（语义正确性、Mathlib复用、跨文件复用）来超越编译指标评估形式化质量。通过对比分析揭示了编译通过但语义不忠实的常见模式（如不完整的多部分陈述、弱化假设、参数限制），证明基于编译的指标会显著高估形式化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体形式化数学教材的工作局限于mathlib中已有良好表示的数学分支，且仅以内核接受（编译通过）作为成功度量标准。作者希望解决这两个局限性：一是将智能体应用于mathlib中几乎不存在的数值分析领域，测试其从零开发新理论的能力；二是提出更系统、可复现的三维评估框架，超越编译指标来评估形式化质量。

Method: 将代码智能体应用于形式化《常微分方程数值方法》教材（数值分析领域，mathlib中几乎不存在）；提出三维评估框架：语义正确性、Mathlib复用、跨文件复用（采用LLM-as-judge方法）；将该框架应用于自己的形式化结果以及RepoProver和M2F的输出，对比分析编译通过与实际质量之间的差距。

Result: 发现了编译通过但语义不忠实的常见形式化模式，包括：不完整的多部分陈述、添加弱化假设、参数限制等。这些模式完全被内核接受（编译通过）指标所掩盖。基于编译的指标会显著高估形式化质量。

Conclusion: 编译通过的形式化并不等于语义正确的形式化，现有基于编译的评估指标严重高估了自动形式化系统的质量。作者提供了可复现的审计方法，以支持未来自动形式化系统更严格的评估。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated that coding agents can formalize entire advanced mathematics textbooks in Lean 4, yet existing efforts concentrate on branches of mathematics already well-represented in mathlib and measure success solely through kernel acceptance. We address both limitations by applying a coding agent to formalize Numerical Methods for Ordinary Differential Equations, a textbook in numerical analysis that is largely absent from mathlib, stressing the agent's capacity to develop new theory from scratch. We further introduce a systematic, reproducible three-dimensional framework for evaluating the quality of agent-produced formalizations beyond compilation: semantic correctness, Mathlib reuse, and cross-file reuse via LLM-as-judge methods. Applying this framework to our own formalization and to the released outputs of RepoProver and M2F, we uncover recurring unfaithful formalization patterns, including incomplete multi-part statements, added weakening hypotheses, and parameter restrictions, that kernel acceptance entirely obscures. Our results suggest that compilation-based metrics substantially overstate formalization quality, and we provide a reproducible audit methodology to support more rigorous evaluation of future autoformalization systems.

</details>


### [145] [Applicability Condition Extraction for Therapeutic Drug-Disease Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14031)
*Guanting Luo,Noriki Nishida,Yuji Matsumoto,Yuki Arase*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向治疗性药物-疾病关系的适用性条件抽取任务，构建了首个包含药物、疾病和适用性条件三元组的手动标注数据集（1,119个药物-疾病对），并系统评估了多种现有方法。同时提出了一种增强LoRA以考虑药物-疾病关系的新方法，在多种评估设置下持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学信息抽取方法主要关注药物与疾病之间的关系识别，而忽略了这些关系适用的上下文特定条件，这在临床决策支持中至关重要。

Method: 构建了首个手动标注的药物-疾病-适用性条件三元组数据集；系统评估多种现有方法；提出一种增强LoRA以考虑药物-疾病关系的新方法。

Result: 所提出的增强LoRA方法在多种评估设置下持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 适用性条件抽取对于临床决策支持具有重要意义，所提出的方法在该任务上表现优异，数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Identifying conditions that a certain drug takes therapeutic effect on a target disease is crucial for clinical decision-making support. However, most existing biomedical information extraction methods have focused on identifying only relations between drugs and diseases, while largely overlooking the context-specific conditions where such relations can apply. To address this problem, we introduce the task of applicability condition extraction for therapeutic drug--disease relations from biomedical research literature. We create the first dataset that has manually annotated triples of drugs, diseases, and applicability conditions on biomedical paper abstracts with 1,119 drug-disease pairs. Using this dataset, we systematically evaluate the performance of a range of existing methods. In addition, we propose a new method that enhances LoRA to consider relations between drugs and diseases. Our method consistently outperforms strong baselines across different evaluation settings. The source code and dataset of this paper can be obtained from: https://github.com/guantingluo98/Drug-ACE

</details>


### [146] [FactoryLLM: A Safe and Open-Source AI Playground for Evaluating LLMs in Smart Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14119)
*Yash Pulse,Yong-Bin Kang,Abhik Banerjee,Abdur Forkan,Prem Prakash Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FactoryLLM，一个用于评估基于LLM的检索增强生成（RAG）模型在智能制造故障诊断与恢复中跨机器文档推理能力的开源安全AI平台，通过双评估体系（RAGAS和LLM-as-a-Judge）验证了其有效性，实验表明所有模型接地性得分均高于0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂中故障诊断与恢复面临挑战，关键信息分散在多台机器的操作手册中，而这些机器通过制造过程相互关联，传统方法难以高效整合和推理跨机器文档信息。

Method: 提出FactoryLLM平台，支持用户配置不同LLM，采用检索增强生成（RAG）技术处理多机器文档，通过RAGAS和NVIDIA的LLM-as-a-Judge双评估体系衡量性能，允许运行本地或开源LLM以保障数据安全。

Result: 以自主智能车辆及其移动规划器软件为案例，评估了三种LLM在30个维修查询上的表现（涉及约600页跨机器文档），所有模型的接地性得分均超过0.88，证明FactoryLLM在跨机器文档推理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FactoryLLM为智能制造领域的跨机器文档推理提供了一种安全、开源且有效的评估平台，代码和文档已公开以供社区在特定制造场景中测试使用。

Abstract: Fault diagnostics and recovery in smart factories is challenging because critical information is dispersed across manuals of multiple machines which are interconnected through the manufacturing process. Large Language Models (LLMs) can provide a promising approach. In this paper, we propose FactoryLLM, a safe and open-source AI playground designed for evaluating different LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) models by analysing documents from multiple machines across the manufacturing process. FactoryLLM enables the user to configure the LLM, and assess performance when reasoning over multiple documents, through a dual evaluation setup using both RAGAS and NVIDIA's LLM-as-a-Judge metrics. FactoryLLM is safe because it allows users to run local or open-source LLMs without sharing sensitive industrial data, providing a controlled environment for experimentation. We demonstrate the efficacy of FactoryLLM through a case study which involves an Autonomous Intelligent Vehicle and its Mobile Planner software, evaluating three LLMs across 30 maintenance queries derived from approximately 600 pages of cross-machine documentation. The results suggest that FactoryLLM is effective in cross-machine document reasoning: every model achieved a groundedness score above 0.88. The full code and documentation for community to test FactoryLLM with their manufacturing specific scenarios are publicly available.

</details>


### [147] [VeriGeo: Controllable Geometry Question Generation with Numerical and Analytical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14176)
*Xiaoxian Duan,Zequn Liu,Yingce Xia*

Main category: cs.AI
